(ii) That guard reads the HONEST live window (`dataset.live_end_stamp`), not `overlap_days`: two ceskereality re-posts of one Jablonec flat are sighted three hours apart and both DETECTED gone on 8 September, so the detection clock calls them 27.6 days co-live and let a re-post's storey through as a fact, with the whole seven-advert family as clusterer collateral. (iii) E154's escape is KEPT and sharpened — with the feed unknown, a price that MOVED is one advert at two moments and the storey moved with it (the same Jablonec flat at 6,988,000 and 6,980,000), while a price that did NOT move is two simultaneous statements (Chrudimská 99's two brokerless sreality micro-units at 2,475,000 and 17 m², 5.4 days together, one storey apart). **REFUSED by the same measurement:** placing bazos in the low camp, which the cohort-5 reader's census supports (bazos < sreality on 391 pairs, bazos < realitymix on 152) and which doubles to quadruples the cost — 452 against 184 on cohort 5, 398 against 82 on cohort 4, 209 against 50 on the region. The camps table is left as W16 measured it.
- **E181** **The storey stated OF THE OFFERED UNIT, and the storey written in words.** E166 reads every storey a body prints, as a SET, because a body names the building's storeys as well as this unit's — and that set reading is why one Dašice mill advert offering a space `umístěného v 1.NP (přízemí)` meets the advert offering the space `umístěného v 2.NP`, whose body also mentions a WC `v 1.NP`: {1} against {1, 2} intersect. `subject_floors` reads only what a PLACEMENT clause predicates of the offered thing, so it is strictly narrower than E166 and can never contradict it. Separately, `v přízemí` against `v patře` carries no digit at all and no numbered reader sees it; it needs no camp table either, because `přízemí` is the ground floor on every portal. Both cost almost nothing: 4 / 32 / 19 / 1 / 2 and 0 / 3 / 4 / 1 / 2 certain duplicates on cohort 5 / cohort 4 / cohort 3 / the region / the trial.
- **E182** **Where the parcel IS the object, its area is read exactly — and a truncating carrier is read by its RESIDUE rather than blanked.** Two readings changed. The TOLERANCE: 2 % is what two portals printing one parcel look like and also what the next plot of a parcelling looks like — Ráby's turnkey packages are 981, 998 and 1,001 m² at 10,886,000 / 10,988,000 / 10,999,000 Kč, land at 6,000 Kč/m² plus a 5,000,000 house, each mirrored unchanged on four portals. For a `dům` or a `pozemek` the bar is therefore an absolute metre plus D57's granularity reading: 1,667 against 1,668 is one tape measure and 897 against a rendered 900 is one parcel, while 998 against 1,001 is the next package. The CARRIER: `plot_area` blanks a source whose parser truncates thousands, which is right for a scored feature and wrong for a fact — a truncation removes leading digit GROUPS and never the last three, so two untrusted numbers still prove two parcels when their residues differ. Two ceskereality adverts of Rezidence Loučná carry `estate_area` 227 and 191 at one catalogue price of 9,567,040 for 82 days together, and the blanked carrier is the whole reason they meet. The residue limb is confined to a house or a plot: one ceskereality `estate_area` of 920 against a 14,788 m² commercial parcel is a different measurement the portal put in the same column, not a truncation of it.
- **E183** **A body that prints the seller's whole CATALOGUE says which row is this advert's.** One Morašice sreality advert lists seven parcels as `• 274/8 + 274/13 — 1 458 m² — 3 459 000 Kč`, and `parcel_numbers` returns all fourteen numbers for an advert that sells one plot; the idnes advert of the NEXT plot prints only `číslo pozemku: 274/9 + 274/14`. Read as sets the two always share a number and the parcel fact can never fire. Each catalogue row carries an area and a price, and the advert carries the same two numbers, so the row selects itself — uniquely, or the reader falls back to the union it always read. The widest keyword set comes with the limb, because `číslo pozemku` is the Czech word order the narrow and wide patterns both miss.
- **E184** **A stated COUNT of dwellings is a fact, and it is not D49's refused limb.** D49 refused the bare co-live price contradiction on a mechanism: one Czech advert legitimately carries two prices for one unit at one moment — a freehold 8,350,000 and a co-operative share of 1,670,000, an exekutorská dražba and an asking price. That refusal stands. `Výnosový dům se 2 byty 3+kk` at 11,100,000 against `Výnosový dům se 4 byty 3+kk` at 21,500,000, both on bazos for 7.3 days under distinct reference numbers, is not that case: the two bodies state two different objects. The conjunction is the rule — a count difference is read only where the adverts were on sale TOGETHER at prices on neither's path — and it costs **0 certain duplicates on all five cohorts**.
- **E185** **A price MOVE between two postings that were never on sale together is one price path.** The standing ruling says a re-post at a new price is the same unit, and `price_demonstrated` has always honoured it — but the `price` FACT never did, so one Vysoká nad Labem house re-listed at 13,700,000 after 14,500,000 is split from its own re-post and from every portal that copied either number, and the group breaks along the cut. The waiver is only safe with an identity beside it: the windows must not overlap, the disposition and the storey must agree where both are stated, the bodies or the photographs must be the same advert's (containment ≥ 0.9 or ≥ 3 tight photo files), and BOTH sides must state an area and state the same one. That last clause is E164's rule applied here and the independent hunter is what bought it: `area_rel_diff` abstains on a missing side, and reading abstention as agreement bridged a Pouchovská 2+kk at 18,500 and a Slezské Předměstí 2+kk at 21,000 — two different flats — through a bazos row carrying no area at all. A CO-LIVE pair is untouched, which is D49's mechanism kept intact, and the waiver reaches the PRICE alone: G19114's two Chrudimská 99 micro-units are one storey apart and E180 still separates them.
- **E186** **MEASURED AND REFUSED: the size a body LEADS with.** `printed_area` compares sets, so the advert of one third of a parcel meets the advert of the whole by naming the whole while explaining the split. Read as "what an advert leads with is what it sells" the limb splits **648 / 621 / 386 / 476 / 117** certain duplicates of cohort 5 / cohort 4 / cohort 3 / the region / the trial — two bodies routinely lead with the terrace, the plot or the building where the other leads with the flat — and the ORDER of two figures is not a fact about the object. Worse, the one fusion it was built for it does not read: HK-Zámeček writes its whole-parcel figure as `o celkové výměře 2 195 m²`, which E153's `_BUILDING_TOTAL` deliberately skips as the building's own size, so the limb compared 459 against 732 and fired by accident. The field stays in the tree, off, and the fusion stays on the unfixed list.

**What cohort 6's confirmation named — W19 / S4 (D59, D60)**

The cohort-6 confirmation put S3's SAFETY beyond question on unseen ground — one fused group of 4,301 against g7's seven, and 0 DIFFERENT in a blind read of 160 new merges — and put its RECALL at 79.3 % against a bar of 85 %. Where the misses are was measured before anything was built (`s4/why_lost6.json`, `why_band6.json`, `why_cluster6.json`): **3,430 of the 5,698 certain duplicates neither generation recovers have both sides known only to the obec**, and the four rules below are what that attribution named.

- **E190** **A camp table fitted on the FLOOR may forgive the same convention in `total_floors` — signed, and only where the stated floors do not contradict it.** `joint_convention_shift` (E133) reads the two numbers as one only when BOTH move together, which a house advert can never do: neither side fills the floor column, so the offset has nothing to travel with, and a one-storey gap in the building's own height is charged as a fact against a pair that agrees on everything else. Measured on the certain duplicates of all six cohorts (`s4/total_camps.json`), a cross-portal `total_floors` gap is rare (90–95 % exact) and, where it exists, it points one way: **idnes states one storey FEWER than sreality on 386 of 5,073 pairs and one MORE on 10; fewer than realitymix on 300 of 3,298 against 5; fewer than mmreality on 354 of 520 against 0** — the same low camp `floor_camps` already places idnes in. So the excuse is the offset the table predicts, in that direction and that size, and a gap the other way or two storeys wide stays a fact; so does any gap between two adverts of ONE portal, which share a vocabulary by construction. Where both adverts DO place the unit the two numbers must tell one story: a floor gap that is neither zero nor the same offset closes the excuse. It buys **903** certain duplicates over cohorts 3–6 and touches **489** structural negatives — every one of which is a `neg_stated_area_conflict`, separated by the area it states and not by the storey count (`s4/e190_negative_rules.json`), which is why the engine still carries 0 of them. On cohort 6 it is the single largest named cause of loss at BOTH grains: 220 gate demotions, 72 promotion refusals and **558 of the 2,527 merge-zone pairs the clusterer split** (M380).
- **E191** **A promotion warrant may rest on evidence only ONE unit has, and not only on the count of public attributes both adverts state.** E131's rail asks for two of nine agreeing attributes — area, disposition, storey, total storeys, price, street, stated area, orientation, room photo — as a proxy for "an operator could check this by reading them". On a corpus of prose adverts that proxy is wrong: **2,118 of cohort 6's 5,698 unrecovered certain duplicates are band pairs stating FEWER THAN TWO of the nine**, because they are bazos re-posts whose whole content is their body — no area, no price, no storey, no street — while only **279 of those 3,507 band pairs carry no `strong_corroboration` at all** (`s4/why_band6.json`), and 2,145 of the 2,321 at obec grain are SEQUENTIAL postings. So the rail gains E164's bar as an alternative: the seller's own order code, three tight non-catalogue photo FILES with the interiors holding, or a body one advert essentially IS. The body limb carries the standing ruling's own shape with it — two postings never on sale TOGETHER — because two adverts alive at once sharing a body are the developer's template (the Černovírské zahrady parcelling), which is the one thing this may not promote on. A warrant is not a merge: `distinguishing_facts` has already found nothing and `demonstration_refusal` still reads every key fact afterwards, so D50 keeps deciding and this limb only decides what D50 is allowed to judge. Obec-grain recall on cohort 6: **72.7 % → 87.0 %** (M381).
- **E192** **Where an advert states no area, the seller's order code or its own body stands in its place.** E185 admits a price MOVE between two postings that were never on sale together, and E164's rule made it ask for an area on BOTH sides — bought by a real bridge, a Pouchovská 2+kk at 18,500 joined to a Slezské Předměstí 2+kk at 21,000 through a bazos row carrying no area at all. The clause is right about abstention and wrong about identity: a bazos row with no area, the same `ev. č. 655820` on both sides and an identical body is one advert re-posted, and that is what split the Penzion "Hámerská jizba" into halves of 46 and 39. A missing area is now waived by a shared rare order code or a body at the containment E185 already trusts, and by nothing else — two STATED areas that differ still refuse, which is the bridge's actual mechanism. **And it is the smallest of the four by far, measured honestly**: on cohort 6 it moves 13 pairs into the merge zone and **0** co-clustered certain duplicates, 0 structural negatives and 0 confirmed fusions — the two Hámerská halves become one group of 84 under S4, but the ablation shows it is E193 that re-unites them, not this limb. It ships ON because the operator's ruling asks for the reading and it costs nothing, and the measurement is on the record so a later wave can take it back out (M382).
- **E193** **A cut the re-partitioner made too early is offered its whole-cell join again, once the local search has finished.** E137's greedy pass reads the cells as they stand BEFORE the search, so a join it refuses is refused against members the search is about to move away; E156's reconcile cannot repair that, because it moves one member per cell per round and two cells of twenty never meet a member-sized move. Every invariant here is monotone — a subset of a valid set is valid — so a union the invariants accept is strictly better than the cut, and the join is offered in `edge.rank` order, certificates first, which is the whole of W13's complaint about the unordered form (one Prostějov component losing its certified member to a cell of three). **And it reads the PROMOTION bar, not the gate's**: a join offered a second time is a merge made on the absence of a fact, which E136 names as the one place the engine has no positive evidence to fall back on — read at the gate's 8 % area instead, it re-opened cohort 4's one confirmed fusion that S3 cures (c4/342450, two Otýlie Beníškové commercial units at 145 m² / 11,338,198 and 139 m² / 10,838,198, whose cells S3 held apart by arrival-order residue rather than by evidence). On cohort 6 it removes the `repartition` invariant from the conflict census entirely, **1,153 → 0**, and the ablation puts it at **267 co-clustered certain duplicates** with 0 structural negatives and 0 confirmed fusions carried — including the two Penzion "Hámerská jizba" halves, which it is E193 and not E192 that re-unites into one group of 84 (M383).

**What S4 does NOT fix, named.** After the four limbs, cohort 6's unrecovered certain duplicates fall from 7,020 to 4,612, and the residue is dominated by ONE shape: **1,084 of the 1,802 pairs neither S4 nor g7 recovers are refused by the `price` fact at CLUSTER grain** — a re-post whose two halves are clean (Mnichov 7,990,000 → 6,990,000, non-overlapping, E185 admits it) but whose cross-portal MIRRORS are still being sighted on both sides of the cut, so some cross-cell pair is co-live at two prices and D49's mechanism refuses the union. Widening the price excuse to co-live pairs is exactly what D49 refused on a mechanism, so S4 leaves it; a wave that fixes it owes a reading of the stale mirror, not of the price. The rest: 259 pairs blocking never retrieves, 218 the model rejects outright, and 219 obec-grain band pairs the D50 A-limb refuses on a stated area, price or town that genuinely disagrees.

- **E200** **A conflict between two of one seller's printed ORDER CODES is NOT a fact — the rule and the band meant to rescue it are both refuted, and the dial ships OFF.** E60 reads a SHARED rare order code as identity, and the mirror claim looked safe: one agency does not give one flat two order numbers at once. Measured on the certain duplicates of all seven cohorts (`s5/codesweep.json`), **263 of them carry two DISJOINT order codes while live together on ONE portal** — trial 12, region 50, cohort 3 12, cohort 4 30, cohort 5 41, cohort 6 94, cohort 7 24 — because a trader who re-posts an advert is renumbered, and the standing ruling says a re-post is the same unit. What those 263 have that cohort 7's CL10850 has not is that the two bodies are the SAME TEXT, so the reading was banded: the masked-token overlap of the two bodies had to be above a floor (one seller's template) and below a ceiling (not one advert twice). The band does not separate the two shapes. Read over every pair S4 co-clusters, it flags **42 pairs across the seven cohorts, of which 19 are certain duplicates** (cohort 5 11, region 4, cohort 4 2, cohort 6 2); hand-read, the same-category refusals are one object re-pitched under a second advert number — `rodinný dům` against `chatu` for one 34 m² Čeperka cottage at 1,200,000 (`N117852` / `N117854`, bodies 90.3 % one text), one 145 m² Plzeň shop whose second posting adds the street-works note (`944918` / `945479`, 85.9 %), one Pardubice villa offered `jako sídlo firmy` and then `pro zdravotnické zařízení s 6 ordinacemi` (`656917` / `657002`, 89–90 %) — while **CL10850's two Purkyňova flats sit at 86.8 % and 87.6 %, inside the same band**. A reading that splits 19 certain duplicates to buy 23, only two of which are the fusion it was built for, is not a reading. `d43_agency_code_conflict` ships FALSE with the measurement beside it; the reading and its tests stay, so the operator can rule on it with one line.
- **E201** **The storey written as an ORDINAL WORD, read WITHIN ONE NOUN and never for a whole building.** `ve třetím patře` carries no digit, so `printed_floors`, `subject_floors` and `ground_or_upper` were all blind to it, and a Czech letting agent writes the storey that way as often as with a numeral: two `ul. Stará` 1+kk of 34 m² let by one Ústí broker are `v přízemí` at 5,125 Kč with a shower and no balcony and `ve třetím patře` at 6,355 Kč with a bath and a `prostorný balkon`, seventeen hours apart so no co-live limb reaches them (cohort 7 CL7958, the one fusion no earlier reading could name). Two guards make the word safe. **The noun**: `3. patro` is the fourth storey and `3. NP` the third, and a writer who means one and types the other is commonplace — one Bílina agency re-writes its own advert for č.p. 707 from `v šestém patře osmipodlažního objektu` to `ve 6. nadzemním podlaží` at the same 8,556 Kč, and one Prague 1+kk is `ve druhém patře` on sreality and `v prvním podlaží` on bezrealitky at the same 20,553 Kč, the patro adding one storey and the two portals' own camps the other. So a storey carries the noun that produced it and only a gap WITHIN one noun is read. **The building**: a house is sold with every storey it has, so a storey its body names is a room's address and not the offer's — one Abertamy 8+1 is re-listed with `V prvním patře se nachází dvě samostatné místnosti` rewritten as `V přízemí najdete dvě samostatné místnosti`, one house, one broker, one price path. The worded readings are not read at all where either side is a `dům` or a `pozemek`.
- **E202** **The plot the BODY states, for the portals that fill no plot column at all.** `plot_area` (E182) reads a stored column and bazos fills none, so one Hrobčice/Razice house offered by ONE seller 25 minutes apart — `+ areál o rozloze 2 830 m²` at 7,999,000 Kč against `+ pozemek o rozloze 1 483 m²` at 6,190,000, 36.9 days on sale together — carries its whole difference in the prose and in the URL slug, and every numbered reader passes it (cohort 7 CL193011; g7 fuses the same two packages on its sreality/idnes rows, cluster 68071). Under D43 two offers with a different stated plot AND a different price, live together, are two objects even where the house between them is one. The noun set is deliberately narrower than `_AREA_SCOPES`'s `land`: a `zahrada` is not the plot, and both of those bodies print the same 87 m² garden terrace, which a set reader would have met on. The conjunction with a price contradiction is what keeps it honest — the re-post of one package two months later carries the same 1,483 m² at the same price and is untouched.
- **E203** **A stated service advance or deposit that differs BESIDE a co-live price contradiction.** D49 refused the bare co-live price limb and that refusal is kept intact: this limb never fires on a price gap alone, because a body may explain a gap (a co-operative share against a freehold, an auction against an asking price, a portal that has not re-read a cut). A SECOND stated number of the same tenancy is not an explanation. One Bílina 2+1 on ul. Bezejmenná prints `Nájemné je 11200 Kč + zálohy na služby 3800 Kč`; another, live with it on bazos for 8.0 and 12.8 days, prints `Nájemné: 9.000 Kč / Zálohy na služby: 4.500 Kč / Vratná kauce: 20.000 Kč / Provize RK: 6.000 Kč`, a different interior and no street (cohort 7 CL319881). A landlord quotes one advance per flat; two advances at one moment are two tenancies. Singleton against singleton only, and the multiplier a deposit clause states first (`kauce ve výši 3 nájmů tj. 15 000,- Kč`) is not a sum of money.
- **E204** **The advert that says its own plot is cut from a parcel the other advert sells whole.** `printed_area` compares SETS and the PART names the WHOLE it will be cut from — that is what makes it a part — so one HK-Zámeček body offering `stavební pozemek o výměře 732 m², určený pro realizaci jedné části moderního trojdomu` and explaining that it `vznikne rozdělením parcely o celkové výměře 2 195 m² na tři části` meets the advert of the 2 195 m² parcel itself on the number that is not the offer, and BOTH rows carry the portal's 2 195 in the area column so no stored reading can separate them either (cohort 5 CL183452, the fusion S2, S3 and S4 all carried). E186's `d43_offer_area` was built for this shape and refused, because reading the LEADING area at any gap costs 648 certain duplicates on cohort 5 for one fusion. This limb asks a narrower question with no tolerance in it: one side states the division and the other does not. Three parts of one trojdům state it both ways round and no stated fact then tells them apart, which is D43 exactly.

- **E210** **`N. patro` and `(N+1). nadzemní podlaží` are ONE storey, and what the two bodies NAME is the veto on every worded storey split.** `same_form_floor_gap` (E166) refuses to convert between the two nouns because a writer who means one and types the other is commonplace — but that refusal is about a DISAGREEMENT, where the module cannot say which storey `3. patro` is against `3. NP`. An AGREEMENT needs no converter it does not already trust: the reader converts `patro` to the NP scale on the way in, so `7. patře` and `8. nadzemním podlaží` are the same number. Five adverts of one 57 m² Brno office at OD Centrum, Kobližná 24, at 7,900 Kč: the sreality body states its own storey in `patro` and the BUILDING's storeys in `NP` (Lidl on 1. PP, 1. NP and 2. NP, Sinsay on 3. NP), so E201's within-noun reading saw `np{1,2,3}` against the realitymix `np{8}` and called it a five-storey gap — the ONE group in S4's whole 239-group floor family whose gap exceeded the portal convention, and it was S5's own creation. The veto reads `printed_floors` for BOTH the set reader and the placement reader, because the worded ordinal is the weakest storey evidence in the stack — it cannot even carry a one-storey claim (E201) — and must not out-vote a storey the two bodies plainly share: it is also what restores the 300 m² Masarykova čtvrť villa let over 2. NP, 3. podlaží and 4. patro, whose placement clauses picked a different storey of one offer on each side and which was S5's only certain-duplicate cost on cohort 8. Behind `d43_floor_cross_form_agreement`; it only ever REMOVES a split.
- **E211** **The size a commercial body LEADS with, read against that advert's OWN stored column — on one portal, live together.** E186's bare comparison of two leads is refused and stays refused: two adverts of one offer routinely lead with different parts of it. A lead that contradicts THIS advert's own column is a different claim — the portal measured one space and the seller is offering a slice of it. Two Brno Business Park adverts of one sreality template, 116 days together, print `Kanceláře o ploše přibližně 860 m²` and `přibližně 450 m²` under one stored 860 and one sentence `Je možné oddělit plochu od cca 450 m² a více`; one Brno-Přízřenice pair prints `K pronájmu jsou tři kanceláře o velikosti 18 m², 22 m² a 35 m²` at 6,500 Kč against `Předmětem pronájmu je hala o ploše 291m2` at 50,000 Kč plus a 2,284 m² yard at 55,000, under one stored 291 on both rows. ONE PORTAL on both sides is the bar that makes it safe: idnes prepends its own generated title — `Pronájem kanceláře 235 m², Brno` — so the idnes lead is the stored column echoed back rather than anything a seller wrote, and against a sreality body that opens on one room of the same let that echo contradicted **44 certain duplicates of cohort 8 alone** (M443). Behind `d43_headline_vs_column`, `_same_source_only`, `_colive_only`.
- **E212** **The storey a letting states as the OFFER.** `subject_floors` (E181) needs a placement verb and reads a twelve-character window after it, so it sees none of `Pronájem přízemního podlaží` (the offer IS the storey, not a unit placed on one), `Místo o ploše 14 m² se nachází v nejžádanějším přízemí` (an adjective stands in the window) or `se nachází v 3 nadzemním podlaží` (the noun is longer than the window). All three name the storey of the offer as plainly as `v 2.NP` does. One Brno-Tuřany office block is let a storey at a time by one idnes broker — `Pronájem přízemního podlaží ... Jedná se o přízemní podlaží samostatného dvoupodlažního objektu` at 72,420 Kč against `možnost pronájmu samostatného 1. patra ... dalších 137m²` at 72,885, both stored at 300 m², 78 days together; one Voroněžská parking house sells three spaces, `v nejžádanějším přízemí` against `v 3 nadzemním podlaží`, both 14 m² at 680,000 Kč. Read on the NP scale (`přízemí` is 1) and judged by the SAME convention as every other storey: two storeys anywhere, one only inside one feed. The cue is narrow on purpose — a placement verb is read only when the clause's own subject is the offered object, so `vývěsky ... umístěné v přízemí vstupu do domu` is a notice board and not the let. Behind `d43_offered_storey`.
- **E213** **The ground-floor convention belongs to the AGENCY, not to the agent.** E145 put it on the portal, E154 moved it to the broker feed — and a broker key is one PERSON. One landlord's portfolio is posted by whichever of its people is free: two 41 m² 1+kk at Vídeňská 1014/18b, both sreality, both `Garantovaný nájem` (firm 2058), carry `broker_key`s 15c7 and 2a10, and `Byt se nachází v 1. nadzemním podlaží` against `ve 2. nadzemním podlaží` was forgiven as a vocabulary difference that one agency, writing one sentence twice, cannot have. `firm` only ever ADDS to `broker`: an unknown key still fails closed (E154 — bazos, bezrealitky and maxima name no broker at all), and the rule fires only where BOTH agencies are known and equal. Reading an UNKNOWN firm as one feed instead was measured and refused: it costs 19 certain duplicates of cohort 8, every one a same-portal one-storey gap at one price and one area, and buys one group (M447).
- **E214** **A designator printed under an explicit unit-identity LABEL is read whatever its shape.** `printed_unit_codes` (E161) requires a digit, because a bare letter is a building and a bare word is prose. A LABEL removes that doubt: an advert that writes `ID jednotky: DOUBLE B` has said the value identifies the unit. One Brno Dornych co-live residence rotates `DOUBLE A`, `DOUBLE B`, `STANDARD` and `STANDARD+` through one idnes slot at 16,900 / 16,900 / 16,490 / 17,390 Kč for 57 days, and every reader on record was blind to all four. Behind `d43_labelled_unit_ids`.
- **E215** **The cellar the body states.** `printed_area` scopes an accessory's m² OUT of the headline comparison — a cellar is not what the flat is sold by, and that scoping is right — and leaves it read by nothing at all. Two Želivecká 4+kk of one six-flat house, same 90 m², same 3. NP, same 10,900,000 Kč, live together on sreality and on ceskereality for 30 and 71 days: one states `dva sklepy o celkové ploše 9 m²`, the other `dva sklepní prostory ... Celkem 10 m² úložného prostoru`. Read as a rounding (9 against 10 at zero decimals is not one number written twice) and only on co-live adverts. Behind `d43_accessory_area`.
- **E216** **The capacity a let states, in the two vocabularies the reader was blind to.** `capacity_counts` (E142) is Czech and office-shaped. **English:** a serviced-office operator publishes one building's products in both languages — Regus Spielberk at Holandská 878/2 prints `soukromá servisovaná kancelář pro 1 osobu` on two rows and `private serviced office space for 2 workstations ... ideal for 2 employees` on the other two, all four at 50 m² and 8,190 Kč, co-living on sreality AND on bezrealitky. **Furniture:** a furnished let sells its unit types by how many people they sleep, and says so in its equipment list rather than in an office phrase — two Dornych co-live units run one 1,900-character template and differ under `Vybavení:`, `2 jednolůžka s úložným prostorem, 2 pracovní stoly` on floor 4 at 14,990 Kč against `postel s úložným prostorem` on floor 3 at 16,690. The bed limb reads ONLY inside that section: a `postel` in prose is furniture in a caption, not the offer's size. Behind `d43_capacity_english` and `d43_stated_beds`.
- **E217** **The obec the BODY names, against the place the other advert is FILED under.** E135 refuses the raw column conflict — portals file a village under its town and the two columns then disagree about one property — and that refusal STANDS (D64). The body is a witness the column is not, under three conditions. The speaker's body must name the speaker's own MUNICIPALITY, so it is talking about itself and at the grain E135 refused: a body naming a QUARTER says nothing about which town it is in, and two byte-identical sreality adverts for one Plíže garage, both `v Maloměřicích`, filed by the portal under Brno-Maloměřice and Brno-Židenice, are E135's own case wearing a body (M451). The OTHER side must be known down to its part, so a village filed under its town cannot look like a different place. And the speaker's names must miss both of the other's. One Brno developer builds one design twice: `v novostavbě domu se třemi samostatnými jednotkami v Újezdu u Brna` at 9,499,000 Kč, and the same 5+kk of 126 m² at the same 9,499,000 filed at Brno-Chrlice, 8.4 km away, whose own body says only that the SHOW home stands in Újezd — `Vzorová dokončená nemovitost se nachází v Újezdu u Brna. V Újezdu u Brna zároveň nabízíme k prodeji dokončené jednotky tohoto projektu`. Behind `d43_body_obec`.
- **E218** **The land a body states: the measurement written before the noun, and the row a seller's own price list assigns to this advert.** Two limbs, one carrier. **The phrasing:** `_PROSE_PLOT` (E202) wants the measurement word AFTER the noun (`pozemek o výměře 732 m²`), and one Brno developer writes it the other way round — `Celková plocha pozemku činí 1.288 m²`. Eight adverts of two Rebešovice halves of one semi-detached pair carry `1.288` and `1.294` in prose while EVERY portal stores the same 1,288 in the column and the same 156 m² of floor; read at E182's exact bar, because 6 m² is 0.47 % — inside the 2 % tolerance and outside what one parcel looks like measured twice — and without the co-live and price-gap limbs E202 carries, because where the parcel IS the object its size is a measurement and not a moment. **The price list:** E183 reads a parcel catalogue keyed on parcel NUMBERS, and one Ochoz u Brna seller's bazos body has none — it prices four plots by name, `Obora (4840 m2) ... Cena za pozemek 958.000,- Kč` — while the portal stores ONE of those prices per advert. Two of that seller's adverts, live together 10.7 days, carry 1,880,000 and 958,000: the 818 m² building plot and the 4,840 m² game enclosure. Behind `d43_prose_plot_exact` and `d43_priced_land_rows`.
- **E219** **A stated difference between two plots whose bodies BOTH say a neighbouring plot is on offer.** D63 keeps the general refusal intact: an attribute one body prints and the other does not is not a fact, because absence is not a statement. What lifts it here is that both sellers SAY there are two — `Současně je nabízen také bezprostředně sousedící stavební pozemek o stejné výměře 500 m². Oba pozemky lze ... koupit společně jako celek o celkové výměře 1 000 m²` — so the one printed difference names which is which. Ten Opatovice adverts of ul. Velké dráhy, all 500 m², all 3,990,000 Kč, live together 09-02..09-22 on four portals, split into two families by one sentence: `Významnou výhodou tohoto pozemku je již vybudovaná elektrická přípojka s osazeným elektroměrem` on one family, `vedení nízkého napětí` on the hranice on the other. It is the pair the cohort-8 blind read called DIFFERENT (card 120) and no reader on record reached. Both sides land, live together. Behind `d43_neighbour_plot_attribute`.

- **E220** **Two lets of one house, on sale TOGETHER, parted by one thing each body states.** D49 refused the bare co-live price gap and that refusal stands: this reading never looks at a price at all. It looks at the second numbers of a tenancy and at the fabric of the flat, and the CO-LIVE WINDOW is its whole guard — a trader who re-posts a let with a new deposit gets a second advert of ONE flat, and the two are never on sale at the same moment, while two flats of one house are. The named list is closed and every item was measured alone over all nine cohorts: the service advance and the deposit (`Služby včetně elektřiny a topení: 3.900 Kč` / `Kauce: 36.000 Kč` against `3.660` / `39.000`, two 2+1 of 55 m² of one Cihelní serviced residence, both at 12,000 Kč, three days together on ONE portal), where the lavatory is (`koupelnu se sprchovým koutem a samostatnou toaletu` against `...a toaletou`, two Vnější 2+kk uploaded two SECONDS apart by one agency), the tense of a refurbishment and what is under foot (`krásné broušené parkety` + `Původní dveře byly citlivě obnoveny` against `Byt BUDE po rekonstrukci` + `novými plovoucími podlahami`, two 75 m² 3+kk of Přívozská 154/9, 42 days together across two portals), and the storey a parking space is on (`v přízemí bytového domu` against `v 1. PP`, two 18 m² spaces of one Provaznická garage at 1,500 Kč). The window is **0.25 days on W8's SIGHTING clock**, swept at 1 / 0.5 / 0.25 / 0: at 0 days the cost jumps to 111 certain duplicates over the nine cohorts, and at 1 / 0.5 / 0.25 it is the same single flag — a pair the cohort-9 blind read had already called DIFFERENT — while 0.25 is the only one of the three that reaches the Provaznická garage pair, whose honest overlap is 0.42 days. The portal scope is per limb, and measured: the NUMBERS are read on one portal only (across portals a service advance is a convention, and reading it costs 3 to 70 certain duplicates per cohort), what is under foot, the tense of a refurbishment and the storey a garage space is on are read ACROSS portals at 0 certain duplicates, and where the lavatory is is not (D66: one Lázně Kynžvart 1+1 is `oddělené WC` on two portals and `WC s integrovaným umyvadlem` on the other two — 3 certain duplicates for one group it would have cured). Behind `d43_rental_colive` and one dial per item.
- **E221** **The four guards a tenancy's second number needs to be one.** Each came from a pair the nine cohorts produced and each is a dial. **The honest clock** — `overlap_days` reads `inactive_at`, which is when a delisting was DETECTED, so two Pekařská postings eleven days apart looked 27 days co-live; `_honest_overlap_days` reads the sighting and they are sequential. **A cap at six times the rent** — one Plzeň bazos body prints its own advert number beside `kauce` and 939,448 Kč is not a deposit on a 105,765 Kč let. **A service advance below the rent** — one Olomouc body prints the TOTAL beside `zálohy` and 18,850 is the rent read twice. **An estimate refused** — `předpokládané měsíční zálohy na energie: 5.000–8.000 Kč` is a range and its two ends are not two tenancies (one Plzeň house let once as a house and once as offices, the sanctioned dům↔komerční cross-type). **And the two rents must MEET** — a deposit of 27,000 at 13,500 against 24,000 at 12,000 is two months both times: it is D49's refused price gap wearing a second number. Together they take the limb's cost from 6 certain duplicates over the nine cohorts to **1**, and that 1 is `507689 x 511488`, which the cohort-9 blind read had already called DIFFERENT.
- **E222** **The unit code nobody wrote in Czech, read per KIND — and the building a portal files in its own url.** `printed_unit_codes` (E161) is anchored on a CZECH unit noun and needs two segments, so the whole of `Hala M2 / Unit NJ1 - 4084 m2` is invisible to it; two adverts of the DOV Vítkovice park are both carried at the park's 2,872 m² headline and part only there, one on `Unit NJ1` and the other on `Unit NJ2`. Read as ONE set the two meet on the hall, which is exactly the thing they share, so the English forms are read per kind (`unit` / `hall` / `building`) and compared kind by kind. The other carrier is the url: realitymix files three commercial units of one Velká Polom development under a byte-identical project blurb and tells them apart only in the slug (`-c1-nebytovy-prostor-103-m2-2211-` against `-b1-...-113-m2-1211-`), and one Rezidence Důl Michal flat prints `budova A2` in its body while realitymix files its neighbour as `-budova-b2-`. The two carriers hold ONE fact, so the slug codes and the body's `building` codes are read as one set — separating them would mean a code never met a code. The slug's own AREA is **refuted**: it costs 43 certain duplicates over the nine cohorts even restricted to one portal (236 on cohort 4 across portals), so `d43_slug_area` ships OFF and the third Velká Polom pair — two `C1` units of 103 m² and 58 m² — stays joined. Behind `d43_unit_codes_english` and `d43_unit_codes_slug`.
- **E223** **D61 stands: two order codes are not a fact BY THEMSELVES — what lifts them is what the BODY offers the space for.** 263 certain duplicates of the nine cohorts carry two disjoint order numbers while live together on one portal, because a trader who re-posts gets a new advert number, and the mirror claim stays refuted. Two Masarykova třída adverts of the Opava MG Medical centre are both 25 m² in the 1. NP at 20,000 Kč, both live on bazos, and print `Evidenční číslo: 933144` against `933138` — and one offers the space `k využití pro obchodní, poradenské, či podobné využití` while the other offers it `k využití jako menší kavárna` that `bude mít svůj samostatný vchod, s případnou předzahrádkou`. The second difference may NOT be the portal's own subtype: one Poniklá chalet is filed as `chata` on one row and `restaurace` on the next by the same seller, and reading the subtype costs 0/5/1/6/6/9/1/0/1 certain duplicates over the nine cohorts where the offered-use lists cost **nothing on any of them**. Behind `d43_agency_code_with_difference` + `d43_offered_use_conflict`; the bare co-live subtype limb is measured (4..125 per cohort) and **refuted**.
- **E224** **The plot attribute a seller picked from a dropdown, and the two contracts that stand in for the co-live window.** bazos prints a seller's chosen value as a labelled line, and one Vávrovice agency is selling at least four 500 m² parcels at 925,000 Kč under one template: `Sklon pozemku: mírný svah` against `Sklon pozemku: rovina`. The vocabulary per attribute is CLOSED (a body that answers `rovina až mírný svah` says nothing) and both sides must state the same attribute, because D63's refusal of the one-sided attribute is untouched. The width is read as a noun AND as an adjective — one Klimkovice seller writes `šířku 28,5 m, na konci 17,3 m` for one parcel and `široká 25,65 m, na opačném konci 17,27 m` for the next. The co-live window has one escape and only one: two DISJOINT order numbers. D61 says a code cannot make two adverts two objects, but two codes do say the second posting is a second CONTRACT rather than a re-post of the first — `Číslo zakázky: 135653` comes down the day `135656` goes up — and that is the only thing the window was standing in for. 0 certain duplicates over the nine cohorts, 41 pairs bought on cohort 9 alone, every one of them that parcelling. Behind `d43_plot_attribute_conflict` + `d43_plot_attribute_code_escape`.
- **E225** **The serviced-office PRODUCT an offer leads with.** One operator publishes a desk and a room from the same building under one catalogue body, both carried at 10 m²: `Nabízíme coworkingové prostory v HQ Laso. Můžete okamžitě začít pracovat v naší sdílené kanceláři.` at 3,290 Kč against `Nabízíme soukromé kancelářské prostory pro 2 osoby v HQ Laso.` at 8,090 Kč. It is read off the FIRST SENTENCE only, because the catalogue that follows names every product the operator sells (`pronájem soukromých kanceláří a coworkingových prostor`) and a body that leads with both says nothing. It is read whatever the clock says — the re-post of the private office two months after the desk came down costs nothing extra and is the pair the co-live window could not reach. 1 certain-duplicate flag over the nine cohorts, and that flag is `90478 x 90525`, which the cohort-9 hand read had already called FUSED. Behind `d43_commercial_product_class`.
- **E226** **What one seller says two of its co-live spaces are FOR, with no code to lean on.** E223 needs an order code beside it, because a code is the thing that tells a re-post from a second contract — and idnes prints none. The same two Opava MG Medical units are on idnes too, uploaded 24 minutes apart and live together five weeks, both 25 m² in the 1. NP at 20,000 Kč: one is offered `k využití pro obchodní, poradenské, či podobné využití` and the other `k využití jako menší kavárna` that `bude mít svůj samostatný vchod, s případnou předzahrádkou`. The vocabulary is closed, the comparison is DISJOINTNESS of the two lists rather than a singleton each (a space is offered for several uses at once, and a re-pitch of ONE space repeats its own list and says nothing), and a body that lists more than five uses is advertising flexibility rather than an identity. Read on ONE portal and only where the two are on sale together. Behind `d43_offered_use_alone`.
- **E230** **The SPACE number a commercial letting plan prints, under its own noun and an explicit `č.`** E61's designator reader is anchored on a DWELLING noun and E161's code reader needs two dotted segments, so `prostor č.201` — three digits after a commercial noun — was read by nothing at all. One Dumrealit.cz template runs nine adverts of ONE Na Zlaté stoce administrative building across five portals, two offices of 15 m² at 4,000 Kč + 1,250 Kč, one order number (`Ev. číslo: 652795`) on all nine rows and the same photographs including the same 3 m × 5 m floor plan: `nabídku prostor č.201` `v prvním patře` against `nabídku východním směrem orientovaný prostor č.303` `ve druhém patře`. Both anchors are mandatory and each carries its own failure: without the NOUN the order number itself reads as a space number, without the `č.` a size does. Read on COMMERCIAL rows only — a flat's body that numbers a room is numbering a room inside the offer, which is why `accessory_designators` is scoped the way it is — and a body printing more than three numbers is a letting plan and abstains, because an empty set is never a conflict. There is NO place gate: the engine only ever asks this question of candidate pairs, which already share a place, and `address_block_key` disagrees with itself across portals for this one building (street grain on sreality/bazos/idnes, a RÚIAN key on ceskereality, a pin on realitymix). Read whatever the clock says (D67) — the two Zlaté stoky offices are SEQUENTIAL, unit A down 2026-07-14 and unit B up 2026-09-16, so a co-live scope reaches neither. 5 flagged certain duplicates over the ten cohorts, every one inside the group the cohort's own hazard read called FUSED and its blind read called DIFFERENT, all five carried by the reference on shared FRAMES alone (M502, M505).
- **E231** **Two commercial adverts that each decompose ONE stored column, and lead with different parts.** E153 compares two bodies' area SETS and abstains the moment they meet — which is exactly what two adverts for two halves of one building do, because each prints its own size AND the other half's. One Jindřichův Hradec bakery at Klášterská 75 is let twice on ceskereality under one 460 m² column and one 20,000 Kč rent, 14 shared frames: `Jedná se o prostor v zadní části domu ... o celkové výměře přes 200 m². K přízemí je možno pronajmout i kancelářské/skladové místnosti ve 2. a 3. NP objektu, které nabízí dalších téměř 250 m²` against `Jedná se o prostory s výtahem ve 2. a 3. nadzemním podlaží ... o celkové výměře téměř 260 m². K místnostem je možné navíc pronajmout přízemní prostory ..., které nabízí dalších 200 m² plochy`. What makes this a statement rather than two leads is ARITHMETIC the advert itself supplies (D68): `dalších N m²` says the N is not what this advert offers, and lead + addition comes to the column the portal stored for the whole object (200 + 250 and 260 + 200 against 460). Both bodies must do it, one portal, on sale together, and the two leads must differ by more than the rounding of the coarser. It fires on 0 to 3 adverts per 20,000-row cohort and on no pair any generation co-clusters (M503, M506).

- **E240** **The house number the ADVERT prints for its own street.** W14 refused the house number at 3.7 % of known duplicates and the refusal was right about the COLUMN: `location.house_number` is the RESOLVER's reading of a portal's address line, and it moves between two postings of ONE body — the same Ondříčkova 116 m² let, re-posted on sreality with a byte-identical text, is filed at `2128/12` and then at `1774/28`; the same Kouřimská 1+kk at `2407/7` and then `2185/9`. Re-measured over all ELEVEN cohorts the refusal reproduces exactly: **418 of 15,603** same-street address-grain certain duplicates carry two different stored numbers (2.68 %), and **255 of the 418 differ only on the č.o.** — `701/11` against `701/13`, one house, two entrances (M530). What an advert PRINTS is a different object: `Nabízíme k pronájmu byt 2+1 na ulici Krasnoarmejců 2080/8` against `na ulici Krasnoarmejců 2079/10` is one agency writing about two flats, one with a shower and one with a bath, 11,950 against 12,650. So the reader parses only what a body states, under `č.p.` or beside the advert's own street name, and states it as the number's COMPONENT SET, because both orders are printed — `Freyova 5/236` is the column stored as `236/5`. Comparison is asymmetric on purpose and the asymmetry is the whole safety argument: two printed numbers AGREE when any component meets (a veto, which can only refuse a split) and CONFLICT only when none does (a fact), so a collision on a low č.o. costs a missed split and never a merge. Two guards, each from a shape the cohorts produced, and each of them worth 1 to 7 false splits: **ONE street key of one obec**, because a CORNER building states both its streets (`Svitavská 29/Vranovská 49` in Brno; one Triangl Park advert, `[ID 86300]`, `Byt G28`, 56.11 m², re-posted on bazos with its address rewritten from `Měděná 3061/4` to `Železná 3068/20`), and **two bodies that are not one text**, because a re-post may carry a corrected address (`Hybešova 2090/30` to `2091/30a` in Kuřim, otherwise word for word the same). A body printing more than two numbers is a developer's list of houses and abstains. 8,988 of 192,530 listings print a number; on the 6,662 certain pairs where both print, **6,646 meet and 16 conflict**, and under the two guards the 16 fall to **2** — both of them hand-read as two genuinely different flats (M531, M533).
- **E241** **What the two bodies PRINT outranks what the resolver filed.** A printed agreement is a VETO on E242, not a fact. The same Freyova 1+kk of 18 m² with a 7 m² sleeping loft, free from 31. 8. 2026 at 19,900, is stored at `236/5` and then at `235/7` while BOTH bodies print `na adrese Freyova 5/236` — the flat did not move, the resolver did. **53 certain pairs over the eleven cohorts carry a stored conflict while their printed numbers meet**, and the one false split E242 would otherwise have made is one of them (M532).
- **E242** **The stored house number, read only where it can be the advert's own.** Four guards cut the 418 to **18**, and all 18 were hand-read: **17 are pairs of genuinely different flats in a reference that calls one letting agency's template siblings duplicates**, and 1 is the Freyova re-post that E241 vetoes — **zero false splits** (M533). The guards, each from a shape the cohorts produced. **The č.p. must differ**: two entrances of one house share it, which is 255 of the 418 and is W14's 3.7 % restated. **Both sides at address grain on one street**: a coarser grain has not stated an address. **A price or an area that MOVED**: two adverts agreeing to the last koruna at two numbers are one advert filed twice, and that one guard is what separates the six cohort-11 fusions from the thirteen one-unit groups the same census flagged (M534). **Two bodies that are not one text**, at an overlap below 0.5 — measured, every false split the eleven cohorts produced sits at 0.94 or above and every true one at 0.44 or below. And **rentals only** (D69): a SALE re-posts with a price cut and a rewritten body, so the move guard stops identifying two units there — measured, dropping the rental gate buys `c9/G089` and costs **four** false splits, one Ostrava 3+1 and one Tábor river plot, each re-posted cheaper under a fresh text (M535).
- **E243** **The interior room match, read only inside the cell it was cut in.** `tag_room_clip_min2` was measured at AUC 0.820 in the HAZARD cell and the 0.90 floor was cut there — and the hazard cell is defined as one address point **AND ZERO tight non-catalogue frames in common**, which is the developer-unit shape. The engine applied the floor everywhere. On cohort 11 that is **416 of the 495 address-point certain duplicates S8 refuses** — 84 % of the loss in the ONE stratum where S8 trails g7 (55.3 % against 69.0 %) — every one of them between 0.837 and 0.898 against a 0.90 floor, on pairs carrying 7 to 9 shared frames, and 430 of the 495 are sreality × bezrealitky. Two galleries that hold the same photograph are not in the cell the floor was cut in, and the engine already says so elsewhere: `tight_photo_match` is E131's alternative promotion warrant precisely because one tight non-catalogue frame in common means two galleries photographed one home. So where that warrant holds, the interior floor abstains (D70, M536).

- **E244** **Which room of a PRICED letting plan an advert is.** E230 reads a space number and abstains above three of them, because a body that numbers four spaces is a letting PLAN and a plan must never refuse anything. That refusal stands and it leaves a shape behind: a plan that PRICES each space has stated, per space, an area and a rent — so the advert carrying the plan is one of its rows. One Frýdek-Místek office building on Na Poříčí is let under exactly that plan and the two portals print DIFFERENT plans of it: the bazos list opens `Kancelář č. 302b – 18,51m2, nájemné 4.123,-` and the idnes list omits 302b altogether, offering 109 and 411a instead, while its own headline says `Pronájem kanceláře, 20 m²`. Resolution runs in the order the plan licenses and every step demands a UNIQUE row: the stored column and the stored rent together (18 m² at 4,123 Kč is 302b and nothing else), then the size the body LEADS with (20 m² is 308b) — because the plan an advert carries need not contain its own space, which is the whole shape — then the rent alone. A plan with two 14 m² offices has not said which one, and an ambiguous answer is no answer. It reaches **5 adverts of ONE building across all eleven cohorts**, costs **0** certain duplicates, and separates the pair cohort 11's blind read called DIFFERENT that nothing else reaches (M537). A reading this narrow is stated plainly as narrow: it is the shape, not the reach, that earns it.

**What S9 does NOT fix, named, and one thing the record had wrong.** The record said the house
number could not be read because `house_number_cp`/`co` is NULL on every row of every cohort
(M476). Half of that is true and the wrong half mattered: the JOINED `location.house_number` is
populated, on 16,190 certain pairs across the eleven cohorts — it is simply the RESOLVER's
reading, not a sentence either advert wrote, which is why it moves between two postings of one
body and why W14 was right to refuse it bare. **`c9/G089`** — Krasnoarmějců 2172/15 at 2,790,000
against 2171/17 at 2,720,000, two co-op flats in adjacent entrances — therefore stays whole,
because it is a SALE and the rental gate is what keeps a seller's second posting from splitting
(M535); it is the priced cost of that guard and it is named rather than hidden. **`c9/G251`**
(Jana Maluchy 206/57 against 207/59) is inside E242's scope. **The cohort-3 ledger is untouched**
— 4 cured / 19 broken / 1 whole exactly as under S4, S7 and S8. **cohort5's two broken entries**
and **cohort9's three** stand. E230's `colive` and one-portal scopes stay refused (D67), E231
still changes no partition anywhere (M503), and the Opava bar, the third Velká Polom pair and
`137030 x 139067` are all exactly where S7 left them.

**What S5 does NOT fix, named.** Four things. **CL10850** — the two Purkyňova flats cohort 7 hand-read as a fusion — stays fused, because the only reading that reaches it is E200 and E200 is refuted (D61): the dial is shipped, measured and OFF. **CL548727**, the Ústí Towers 1+kk printed at 20,3 m² / 2,799,000 against 20,5 m² / 2,499,000, stays fused exactly as under S4 — both rows store the portal's rounded 20 m², which `printed_area_conflict` keeps in as a shared reading, and no S5 limb touches it. **The Kolová parcelace (cohort 6 CL160829)** stays fused: the two 1,100 m² plots are the same size, and the 2,770,000 against 1,899,000 is excused because the cheaper advert's own price path ran through 2,770,000 — the only thing that separates them is one clause of prose about who will lay the services, which `aligned_difference` cannot reach because the replaced segment is longer than its window. **And the recall residue is unchanged**: cohort 7's 583 NO-FACT losses against g7 are one advert partitioned along its own descending price path, 30 of 30 hand-read, and S5 adds no reading that bears on them.


**What S6 does NOT fix, named.** Five things. **The cohort-3 ledger is untouched** — 19 broken and 1 whole, exactly as under S2, S3, S4 and S5: those entries are the Olomouc parcelace whose halves share every printed number. **cohort 4's three, cohort 5's two, cohort 6's Kolová and cohort 7's CL10850 and CL548727 stay exactly where S5 left them** — no S6 reading bears on any of them, and none of them regressed. **S6 introduces two false splits on eight cohorts**, both hand-read and both costing no certain duplicate: one `DOMY Nemilany` house re-posted on idnes with the floor column read 1 then 0 and no storey in either body, and one Plzeň Rokycanská shop whose second advert leads with its 145 m² sales floor. **The commercial part-vs-whole that appears only ACROSS portals is unreachable** and deliberately so: idnes writes its own column into the body as a title, so there is no seller's lead left to contradict it (M443). **And the recall residue is unchanged** — cohort 7's NO-FACT losses against g7 are one advert partitioned along its own descending price path, and no S6 reading bears on them.


**What S7 does NOT fix, named.** Six things, and five of them are a refusal rather than a gap. **The Opava bar** (`236356 x 18825855`) — one sreality body that prints `přenechám bar ... cena odstupného 350 tis` and NO area at all against one bazos body that prints `velikost 85 m2, venkovní prostor 150 m2`, both carried at a 100 m² headline — stays joined: the only reader that reaches it is E211 widened past its co-live and same-portal scope, and that widening costs 258 certain duplicates over the nine cohorts. It is the one of the cohort-9 blind read's sixteen that no S7 reading reaches. **The third Velká Polom pair** (`449963 x 456437`) stays joined: both units are filed under `C1` and only the slug's AREA (103 m² against 58 m²) separates them, and the slug area costs 43 even restricted to one portal — the other two pairs of that group part on the building letter, so the group BREAKS rather than being cured. **The two cross-portal Vnější pairs** stay joined: where the lavatory is is read on one portal only (D66), so the group breaks into its same-portal halves. **The three house-number groups** — Krasnoarmějců (`G089`), Jana Maluchy (`G251`) and Nedbalova (`G361`) — stay whole exactly as under S6 and S4: `house_number_cp`/`house_number_co` is NULL on every row of every cohort (M476), and all three are SEQUENTIAL postings, which E220's own construction rule excludes. **The two g7-only groups** `G020` (two streets printed in one body headline) and `G164` (two Muglinov terraced houses) are untouched, as under every generation since S2. **And the cohort-3 ledger is untouched** — the Olomouc parcelace whose halves share everything they print.


**What S8 does NOT fix, named.** S8 is a NARROW wave — it moves exactly one group on one cohort and is byte-identical to S7 everywhere else — so everything S7 left standing stands, and it is named again rather than left to be reconstructed. **The Opava bar** (`236356 x 18825855`) stays joined: only E211 widened past its co-live and same-portal scope reaches it and that widening costs 258 certain duplicates (M475). **The third Velká Polom pair** (`449963 x 456437`) stays joined: only the slug's own AREA parts them, 43 even restricted to one portal. **c9/G020** (two streets in one body headline), **c9/G166** (the Velká Polom building letter) and **c9/hazard/18595975** (the Provaznická parking storey) stay partly broken. **c9/G089** and **c9/G251** — Krasnoarmějců and Jana Maluchy, two co-op flats in adjacent entrances — stay whole, because the fact is the house number and `house_number_cp`/`co` is NULL on every row of every cohort (M476), re-checked this wave on all ten. **c9/hazard/224048** (Brumovice/Uhlířov) stays whole. **The cohort-3 ledger is untouched** — 4 cured / 19 broken / 1 whole exactly as under S4, S6 and S7: the Olomouc parcelace whose halves share every printed number, which under D43 is INDISTINGUISHABLE rather than a defect. **`137030 x 139067`**, the one S7 recall loss no rule explains, is unchanged. And two things this wave measured and refused rather than left: **E230 scoped to co-live** reaches only the row the agency edited in place and leaves the two Zlaté stoky offices fused (D67, cost 1 buy 3), and **E230 scoped to one portal** costs 4 and buys 0.





---

## 2. Q1 — Duplicate definition and hard guards

**Definition (E1).** Two listings are duplicates iff they advertise the same physical unit under the same deal type — irrespective of portal, broker, advert age, price, or photo set.

**In scope:** re-listings across time (E6), same-portal same-broker re-posts (E7), active↔inactive (E8).

**Explicitly not duplicates:** two different units in the same building or development, *even sharing marketing photos, the same site plan, the same address, the same broker and boilerplate text*. This is the hard negative class and the engine is shaped around it.

**The hard guards, restated exactly** (E2, E3), with the tightenings the engine adds because it gates a merge rather than a candidate (E5), and the grain correction (E4).

One note on mechanics: a re-post that reuses the same `source_id_native` is already **one row** via `listings_source_native_uidx` (migration 091). Only new-id re-posts create duplicate rows, which is what the engine is for.

### How re-listings and re-posts are handled

These are mechanically the easiest pairs (byte-identical photos, near-identical text) and the most dangerous (a broker's template is reused across units). The engine therefore never accepts *template evidence* alone. A re-post merges on **unit-specific** evidence: identical or ±1 m² area, identical floor, interior-room photo matches (not exterior, not plan), rare-token overlap in the description. Certificate K-B (E24) handles the clean case without spending a judge; its zero-active-overlap clause is what separates a re-post from a development.

### How developer projects are kept apart — five independent rails

1. **Blocking never lets a shared-photo block explode into pairs** (E17): an exploded photo block is the catalogue detector, and membership becomes a negative feature.
2. **Catalog-photo subtraction before any image feature** (E9), with the leftover ratio fed back as a negative.
3. **Image evidence split by tag family** (E10) — "same building" and "same unit" are *different numbers*, so a shared plan can never be read as unit identity.
4. **The evidence-diversity gate** (E11): images alone can never merge.
5. **The four-way judge verdict** (E27): `same_building_different_unit` becomes a permanent must-not-link and is a far more informative negative label than plain "different", and the prompt is explicitly adversarial about Czech developer practice.

Plus two block-level priors: a block where ≥8 listings share one `broker_identity_id` with pairwise text containment ≥0.7 is flagged `project_suspected`, which raises `T_hi` inside the block and routes every borderline pair to vision; and the assembled negative-control cohort (§3) exists so this failure mode is *measured*, not assumed.

**W15 adds a sixth rail, and it is the one that works where the other five are blind (E140–E143).** The five rails above are all about the PHOTOGRAPHS and the judge; a developer's neighbouring units defeat every one of them, because the photographs really are the same and the judge was withdrawn (D19/D20). What separates them is what the adverts PRINT: the land-register parcel, the accessory number the building itself assigns, how much is on offer, and — where none of those is printed — the fact that one advert has ONE area and ONE price at any moment, so two adverts whose area AND price both moved are two units. On the 140-town region cohort this is what closes the case: every one of the 18 groups g8 fused is separated, and the certain-duplicate recall RISES rather than falling.

**Honest limit.** Shared photos + identical text + identical disposition + areas within 8% + an absent unit number is genuinely under-determined. If the trial shows this, the correct answer is to route the whole `project_suspected` block to propose-only. That costs recall, not precision.

---

## 3. Q2 — Trial region and sample

The corpus is all-time (822,599 listings; 612,076 reach a town), so town counts run ~2.2–2.7× their active counts. Praha alone is 79% of all country pairs even after a 119-quarter split, so it distorts every cost estimate and is excluded as the primary block.

**Ruling D1 fixes the cohort shape — four blocks, sized to budget, final pick after the census:**

1. **PRIMARY** — the top-ranked non-Praha district town, **2,000–3,500 all-time** listings. The regime a rollout would live in.
2. **SPARSE** — one small town, **500–700 all-time**. This block doubles as the **exhaustive O(n²) blocking-recall bound**: at ~600 listings that is ~180k pairs of pure CPU, $0 (the *labels* for it are budgeted; enumerating the pairs is free).
3. **DENSE** — one Praha `cast_obce_kod` quarter, **1,000–1,500 all-time**, chosen for new-build activity (developer stress). `cast_obce_kod` covers 85% of Praha vs `momc_kod`'s 24%.
4. **NEGATIVE CONTROL** — assembled, not geographic, **≤ 800 listings**: every listing in blocks 1–3 that either shares a `ruian_adm_kod` with another listing at a **different floor**, or carries a dHash appearing on **≥ 5 distinct listings**. Free to build from census probe B4. A mandatory evaluation stratum (E23).

Hard filters and the weighted score are specified in full in the CENSUS SPEC: ≥6 of 9 portals; all four `category_main` cells ≥30 rows; both deal types present; ≥70% of listings with a stored image and ≥90% of those with CLIP; size 1,500–5,000; not Praha as primary. **The operator picks the blocks off the census table; the program does not pick for them.**

**Expected pair volume.** The small-town pilot measured 3–11 candidates/listing with a single town+attribute rung; six probes roughly triples that. Expect 12–25 p50 in a district town, 30–60 p99 in the Praha quarter, capped at 60 (E17). At the cohort sizes D1 fixes, that is **~40,000–60,000 distinct trial pairs** (cohort × c / 2 — a pair is scored once, not once per side; the Appendix A convention) — a small artifact, iterable locally at zero cost.

**Iteration plan and what it costs.** The secretless shell is unblocked by **one export dispatch**: W1 ships the whole cohort as a gzipped Actions artifact (fingerprints, scrubbed descriptions, phash lists, snapshot price series, hashed broker keys, location fields, **and precomputed CLIP/pHash cross-evidence for every generated candidate pair**). After that, blocking, features, model fitting, calibration, clustering and evaluation all run **locally at $0**. Photos can be eyeballed locally through the public `GET /images/{storage_path}` → 302 presigned R2, no secrets needed.

Expect **6–10 offline iterations** (free) and **3 judge-prompt iterations** (paid). Paid sequence: I0 census $0 → I1 export $0 → I2 ground truth $32 → I3 prompt iteration $10 → I4 full trial pass + recall probes $60 → I5 shadow week $10 (only if W7 is approved). **~$102 committed of $200**, ~$112 if the W7 shadow week is approved (D2), and **every one of those iterations is a row in `autodedup.iterations`**, which is what the progress page renders (§12).

---

## 4. Q3 — Candidate generation (blocking)

O(block) per new listing (E13, E14), robust to the four structural holes: shared town-centroid pins, missing area, missing broker, missing disposition.

Each listing emits a small set of blocking keys into `autodedup.listing_fp` and `autodedup.image_band`. Retrieval is a lookup of that listing's keys, union of co-members, minus self, capped.

| probe | key | fires when | what it buys |
|---|---|---|---|
| **K1** block+attr | `(block_key, cat_group, category_type)` narrowed by equal `disposition` **or** `area_band ∈ {b−1,b,b+1}` | either attribute present | the workhorse |
| **K2** address | `(obec_kod, street_key, house_number_cp)` | both present (~17.5% corpus) | near-decisive; **rescues attribute-less rows** |
| **K3** broker | `(broker_key, cat_group, category_type, price_decile)` | broker resolved | same-broker re-posts **across town boundaries** |
| **K4** dHash bands | 4 × 16-bit bands of the anchor images' dHash | any phashed image | **location-free**; the only lane that works when every attribute changed |
| **K5** text bands | SimHash/MinHash bands over description shingles | description ≥200 chars | **location-free**; the bazos lifeline |
| **K6** foreign | `(country_code, cat_group, category_type, area_band)` | `country_status='foreign'` | the 45,619 abroad listings are an answer, not a loss |

`block_key` = `cast_obce_kod` in the split cities, else `obec_kod`; `cat_group` folds `{dum, komercni}` into one token so the sanctioned cross-type survives blocking. The **log-band trick** (`band = FLOOR(LN(area)/w)`, `w = −ln(1−t)`, join offsets {−1,0,+1}) is lifted verbatim from `toolkit/dedup_candidates_sql.py` as the *key derivation*, which turns a range tolerance into an equality lookup.

**K4/K5 are deliberately location-free, and that is the design's most important reach decision.** Only 10.2% of the corpus is geo-blockable and 17.5% carries a street key. The engine's reach must not be capped by the location program's reach.

**Anchor-image selection for K4** (`ad_anchor_images`, **3** per D8): the images with the highest *interior*-family tag score, falling back to sequence 0,1,2. Indexing by cover shot instead would bias the whole image lane toward facade and site imagery — exactly the catalogue failure mode. Catalog hashes (E9) are excluded from anchors. **D8 also rules out any DDL on `public.images` in this program** — no new shared-table index of any kind; the image lane runs on what already exists, and if that proves too slow the finding is reported, not patched with a hot-table index.

**The failure modes, answered**
- *Shared town-centroid pins* (122,920 listings on 4,206 pins, largest 1,029): a non-issue by construction — no probe keys on a coordinate (E16). Distance is a masked, uncertainty-normalised feature with a `pin_pop` discount.
- *Missing area* (all 138,997 bazos rows): K1's disposition arm still fires; K2/K4/K5 are unaffected. bazos's `postal_town` disagrees with the geo obec on 57% of rows, so **for bazos the photo and text probes outrank the town probe** — expressed as a data row in `autodedup.settings`, never a branch in shared code (CLAUDE.md rule 21).
- *Missing broker* (bazos, bezrealitky, maxima carry no broker row at all): K3 emits nothing; `broker_known` is a mask feature.
- *Missing disposition* (realitymix's 20,722 town-known attribute-less rows, all land): K1's area arm plus K2/K4/K5. For `pozemek`, area is the only identity attribute anyway.
- *remax*: reach is contradicted between two repo sources and is **re-measured in the census** (probe B2). If still unlocated, remax rides K3/K4/K5.

**Explosion control (E17).** An exploded `attr` block falls through to the next-most-specific key; an exploded *photo* block is retained as the catalogue detector. Fan-out fills in priority order `addr > phash > text > broker > attr1 > attr3`, so the cap discards only the weakest class.

**Expected pairs per listing: 12–25 p50, ≤60 p99.** At 25 candidates a `resolve()` pass is ~6 index probes plus ~25 feature computations — sub-second, which is what would make it a worker lane rather than a batch job.

---

## 5. Q4 — Pairwise features at zero marginal cost

One bulk fetch per block: the listing row (`scraper/db.py` `LISTING_COLUMNS`), the `listing_location` row, images (sequence, phash, storage_path, CLIP vector, tag), the last ~12 `listing_snapshots` price points, broker ids. Order versioned in `autodedup/features.py:FEATURE_ORDER`. All obey E12.

**Scope ruling (program lead).** The catalogue below is ~45 features. **W2 ships a core set of ~25** — the ones with a stated mechanism and no measurement dependency — and the set **grows only by error analysis in W4**: a feature is added because a named error class demands it, and the iteration that added it is recorded in `autodedup.iterations` with the metric delta it bought. Certificates K-A/K-B/K-C (E24) are in the core set.

**Attributes.** `area_rel_diff`, `area_exact`, `area_known_both`; `dispo_equal`; `floor_diff`, `floor_known_both`; `total_floors_equal`; `ownership_equal`, `building_type_equal`, `condition_equal`, `energy_equal`, `furnished_equal`, `subtype_equal`; boolean amenities (`has_lift`, `cellar`, `garage`, `terrace`, `parking_lots`) collapsed into **`attr_agreements`**, **`attr_contradictions`** (both known and different — the strongest single negative in the set) and **`attr_agreements_rare`** (each agreement weighted by inverse in-block frequency, so "both energy B" outscores "both has_lift"). `has_balcony`/`has_parking` are legacy conflated booleans and carry a reduced weight. `area_m2` only — the one headline area since location W17.

**The parcel, and the vocabulary the slots are read in (W4e, `FEATURE_VERSION` 3).** Error analysis put three residual merge-zone errors on a fact no slot compared — `estate_area` and `garden_area` are numeric and sit outside `ATTR_KEYS` — so the v3 tail appends **`plot_area_rel_diff`** and **`plot_area_exact`** (≤2 %), both ATTR, both present only when BOTH sides carry a *trusted* plot. The plot is `estate_area`, and on `pozemek` the headline area (measured identical on 177/177 rows carrying both); `garden_area` is never it (median 0.84 of the estate). Trust is gated **two ways**, because the truncating parsers fail in two shapes (M24). *Per (source, carrier)*, where the population convicts the whole column: five portals parse areas with a regex that has no thousands separator, so ceskereality reads "5 870 m²" as **870** and 0 of its 173 estate values reaches 1,000 where sreality and idnes reach it on 43–45 %. *Per value*, where it does not: bazos is the largest headline carrier the code reads (40 of 70 rows) and does reach 1,000 on 13 of them, so only the listing's own advert can convict the number — a stored plot that is a three-digit tail of a space-grouped number in its own description ("11 197 m²" stored as 197) is dropped, a test that flags 6 of bazos' 40 and **0 of the 431 values on the four clean carriers**. A plot below 10 m² is a data-entry artefact (36 rows carry `estate_area = 1`) and never reaches the comparison; a pair whose smaller plot is exactly the last three digits of the larger is read as ABSENT rather than as a contradiction. `plot_area_exact` is ATTR corroboration, but on **thin and unseparated** evidence: 40 of 42 judged duplicates carrying two trusted plots agree within 2 %, against 3 of 9 non-duplicates — the whole labelled negative set that carries them. Tightening the threshold and gating on plot rarity were both measured and neither separates (the false fires are exact matches on a modal parcel: 740 m² is the median `dum` estate on both sreality and idnes). The rule is kept because it decides nothing today (stripping it moves 0 pairs between zones) and is owed a labelled draw before the refit leans on it. Alongside it, **attribute comparison now runs on canonical values** (`normalize.canonical_attr`): portal synonyms fold onto one token (`ve_vystavbe_(hruba_stavba)` = `ve_vystavbe`, `zdeny` = `cihla`, `velmi_dobry` = `dobry`, `ano` = `castecne`) and "other"/"unspecified" values (`jina`, 123 ceskereality rows) read as absent — each fold measured to raise the contradiction ratio between judged duplicates and non-duplicates (M22). A `false` in a boolean slot from a portal that only ever publishes the positive is a parser default, not a fact, and is dropped (`FALSE_BY_OMISSION`, M23); a portal that *does* publish the negative keeps contradicting.

**Price and price history.** `price_last_ratio`, `ppm2_rel_diff`, `price_magnitude_class_equal` (sale-scale vs rent-scale — the fallback when `category_type` is NULL), and the strong one: **`price_path_event_match`** (E19) plus `price_path_shift_ratio` for the re-listing shape.

**Text (stdlib only).** Reuses `location_data/resolver/normalize.py` (`deaccent`, `normalize_match_key`). `jaccard_5shingle`; **`containment_max`** (catches truncated re-posts, which plain Jaccard misses); `tfidf_cos` over the block corpus; `desc_simhash_hamming`; `len_ratio`; and the discriminators (E20) **`rare_token_overlap`** (in-block df ≤2: street names, unit codes, project names) and **`numeric_fact_overlap`** / **`numeral_conflict`** over `(value, unit)` pairs (m², Kč, NP/patro, unit numbers). Conflicting numerals in the same slot type is itself a reject condition. No MinHash storage beyond the blocking sketch — exact Jaccard is computed on the fly for ≤60 candidates.

**Broker.** `same_broker_identity`, `same_firm`, `same_phone_norm`, `same_email_domain`, `broker_known_both`, `firm_is_franchise` (a franchise email is not an identity), `contact_rarity` (a phone/email with corpus df ≥50 — mmreality's single switchboard across 12 broker ids — contributes nothing).

**Location** — read only from `listing_location` (location lives ONLY there since migration 508). `same_ruian_adm_kod` (address-point identity, near-decisive: sreality 26.9%, bezrealitky 60.7%, idnes 1.4%, bazos 0%); `same_street_key`, `same_house_number_cp`, `same_psc`, `same_cast_obce`; `dist_m` emitted **only** when both pins clear the precision gate, else NULL with an explicit `pin_quality` indicator; **`dist_norm = dist_m / (r_a + r_b + 25)`** so 400 m between two address points is damning and 400 m between two municipal pins is noise; `granularity_rank_min` via `location_granularity_rank` (never the enum's order); `same_exact_pin` paired with `pin_pop`.

**Images** — all computed **after** catalog subtraction (E9). `phash_tight` (Hamming ≤6, `bit_count((a # b)::bit(64))` — with the cast; `bit_count` is not defined for bigint), `phash_loose` (≤11), **`phash_match_ratio` = matches / min(n_a, n_b)** (the ratio carries more signal than the count); `clip_max_cos`, `clip_mean_top3_cos` (11.3M vectors, no ANN index needed at ≤60 candidates × ≤15 images, and **zero storage** — computed lazily per pair); **`seq_monotone_ratio`** (matched pairs preserving gallery order — a re-post keeps order, a catalogue reuse does not); the **three tag-family ratios** (E10); `catalog_ratio`; `shared_photo_df_max`; `n_images_min`.

**Temporal.** `gap_days`, `overlap_days`, `both_active`, `same_source`, and `source_pair` one-hot over the 45 unordered source pairs, which lets the model learn per-portal-pair priors.

---

## 6. Q5 — Decision model

**Layer 1 — the rule floor** (`autodedup/guards.py` + `decide.py`). Pure Python, no training, absolute authority, evaluated first and never overridden. It (a) vetoes on E2–E5 **and on E61's conflicting unit designator**, (b) auto-rejects on `attr_contradictions ≥ 3` or `numeral_conflict`, (c) auto-accepts the certificates — E24's three, plus **E60's K-R, which is read before them** — which also seed the bootstrap positive labels before any judge has run.

**Layer 2 — a calibrated stdlib logistic regression** (E21), over the feature set plus a handful of hand-specified interaction terms encoding the domain physics a linear model cannot see alone: `interior_match_ratio × (1 − shared_photo_block)`, `rare_token_overlap × same_source`, `dist_m × pin_quality_min`, `area_exact × dispo_equal`, `price_path_event_match × gap_days_bucket`, `catalog_ratio × phash_match_ratio`.

Why learned rather than hand-tuned: the interaction between *missingness patterns* and *source pair* is a surface nobody will tune by hand, and it is exactly where bazos, remax and realitymix live. But the model **starts hand-initialised from priors** so W2 can run before any labels exist, and those hand weights remain the fallback if a fit degrades. If measured AUC lands below 0.985, the fallback is ~200 lines of stdlib gradient-boosted decision stumps, still interpretable.

**A fitted model is checked against the CODE at load, not only against itself (W4e).** The `provenance.feature_version` stamp is a digest of the feature order the artifact carries, so comparing it to the artifact is tautological: a model fitted before a feature landed passes the stamp and then scores the new feature at zero, silently. `LogisticModel.check_feature_order` supplies the other half — `score` looks weights up by NAME, so the invariant is membership: a feature name the code no longer defines is a **load-time error**, and a name in `features.FEATURE_ORDER` the model does not carry is the **refit debt** and warns naming every one. Only a stamped (i.e. fitted) artifact is checked; a hand-built model over a deliberate subset is exempt. `w4_gold` carries 45 of the current 47 names and warns about `plot_area_rel_diff` and `plot_area_exact` on every load until it is refitted.

**Three zones** (E22) and **the diversity gate** (E11) — a pair with only one evidence family, however high its score, is forced into the band.

**Calibration and thresholds.** On the sealed, cluster-split holdout: precision(t) and recall(t) with **Clopper–Pearson** exact intervals, **per stratum** (E23/D10). `T_hi` = the smallest t whose **Wilson 95% lower bound on precision ≥ 0.99 with point ≥ 0.995, and no stratum below 0.97** (D3). `T_lo` = the largest t at which cumulative missed positives below it are ≤3% of all positives, **Horvitz–Thompson-weighted** by the known per-stratum sampling rates. `b = P(T_lo < p < T_hi)` is then read off and is the budget dial (E25). Thresholds live on the `models` row, so a threshold change *is* a model version change.
