| M49 | what every judge arm — and every rule over their stored verdicts — is worth on band negatives drawn from MANY developments, and what each costs at M20's dial | D17's rates rested on 18 band negatives spanning 16 listings, 14 of them one operator card, and it ruled that only an operator pass could widen that axis | **the axis widened to 61 band negatives over 14 developments without an operator pass, and it splits the question in two: where the adverts PRINT a conflict every arm is competent, and where they print nothing that separates two units no arm is.** Four arms over M48's 248 structural band pairs (43 negatives / 11 blocks, 205 positives / 48 blocks) plus, as a second reference, W6's own 91 operator band labels (18 negatives / 5 blocks), **$2.7373 total**. On the STRUCTURAL negatives: `qwen3-vl-235b-a22b` strict **0/43, Wilson 95 % [0, 8.20], 0 blocks** ($0.003524/pair, p50 5.8 s) and `qwen3-vl-30b` strict **0/43** ($0.001732, p50 3.4 s), text `gpt-5.6-luna` j2 **3/43 = 6.98 %, 2 blocks** ($0.000758, p50 2.1 s), vision `gpt-5-mini` j2 **6/43 = 13.95 %, 5 blocks** ($0.004404, p50 14.1 s). **Every arm is 0/27 where the two bodies print conflicting unit numbers; every error any paid arm makes is on the 16 stated-area conflicts.** POOLED over both references (61 negatives / 14 blocks, 272 positives / 74 blocks): q235 **1/57 = 1.75 % [0.31, 9.29], 1 block**, recall 162/267; q30 **1/56**, recall 70/259; text **7/61 = 11.48 % [5.67, 21.84], 6 blocks**, recall 247/272; vision **17/61 = 27.87 %**, recall 254/272 — and the operator half is where the damage is (text 1/14 on `same_building_different_unit`, 2/2 on `same_project_different_unit`, vision 10/14). **All 18 operator band negatives print NO separating fact** — no unit designator on either side, no disjoint stated area, 17 of 18 inside one address block — and **no structural rule names a single one of them**, so the two negative sets are disjoint and the judge's residual job is precisely the case structure cannot reach. Of **162 rules scored free** over the stored verdicts (each arm alone, unanimity of 2/3/4, both cascade orders, a self-reported confidence floor, each under the all-arm abstain/same-building veto), the best safety per dollar is not an ensemble: **`text@0.95` — the text arm acting only on its own `same_property` at confidence ≥ 0.95 — makes 2/61 = 3.28 % [.., 11.19] over 2 blocks at 79.8 % band recall for text's own price**, beating `unan(text+vision)` and both cascade orders (4/61) at a fifth of the cost; `text@0.99` is 1/61 [.., 8.72] at 47.1 %. The floor is CHOSEN on the pairs that score it, so its interval is optimistic and it needs a confirmation pass. At M20's production dial (296,550 band pairs a month) nothing else is near the budget: text and every gate on it **$224**, q30 **$507**, `casc(text>q235)` **$1,014**, q235 **$1,030**, vision **$1,314**, any all-arm veto **$3,071**; at D15's 75 %-recall dial text is **$86** and remains the only measured configuration inside $50–150 | judge runs `35490419839` `35490587387` `35490794443` `35491042156` `35491642944` `35492031522` `35492862019`; `w7/judges/{findings,index,masking,confidence,proposal_precision,operator_negative_anatomy}.json` + `{structural,operator,pooled}/compare.json` | W7 |

No negative carries a shared code and **27 of 27 `neg_unit_number_conflict` pairs show a unit designator on BOTH sides**, which is the right exposure for a negative and a confound for a positive. E242's guards fire on **all 6 and on 0 of the 13** — and the guard that does it is the MOVE guard: all six fusions carry a rent or an area that moved with the number (0.6 % to 10.4 %), while eleven of the thirteen agree to the last koruna AND the last m², and the other two are one Ondříčkova 116 m² let re-posted with a byte-identical body at 38,990 then 37,990 (body overlap 1.000, which the INDEPENDENCE guard refuses). | `s9/hnbuy_cohort11.json`, `s9/hnvariants.json` | W24 |
| M535 | what the rental gate is for | — | Without it E242 flags five more certain pairs, of which **four are false splits**: Markova Ostrava 2931/34 against 2942/24, both 70 m² 3+1 with a balcony on the 7th floor of one panel block, sold by the owner, sequential, 5,630,000 → 5,490,000 → 5,450,000 with the body rewritten (overlap 0.215 and 0.058); and Bechyňská Tábor 1572/1 against 393, the same 1,330 m² plot on the Lužnice bank beside the one villa, 7,970,000 → 6,390,000, sequential, body rewritten. A letting agency's second flat is a second CONTRACT; a seller's second posting is the same object cheaper. | `s9/hnfinal.json`, `s9/handread_s9.json` | W24 |
| M536 | the address-point stratum, diagnosed and moved | — | **495** of cohort 11's address_point|address_point certain duplicates were lost by S8, and **416 of them (84 %)** by ONE limb — `interior`, at `room_clip_min2` between 0.837 and 0.898 against a 0.90 floor — on pairs carrying **7 to 9 shared frames**, 430 of the 495 sreality × bezrealitky. The floor was cut in the HAZARD cell, which excludes pairs with tight non-catalogue frames in common, so it was being read where it had never been measured. E243 confines it to its cell: **addr|addr 55.3 → 81.6 %** on cohort 11 (g7 69.0), and it gains on every other cohort too — cohort3 76.4 → 78.8, cohort6 85.1 → 86.4, cohort7 84.9 → 86.4, cohort9 86.2 → 87.7, cohort10 87.1 → 88.0, region 90.0 → 91.5 %. | `confirm_cohort11/losses.json`, `s9/measure_s9.json` | W24 |
| M537 | the hunters re-run over S9's groups, cohorts 10 and 11 | — | **0 introduced false merges.** cohort 10: 40 newly flagged member sets, 2 of them splits, **38 put something together**; cohort 11: 274, 1 split, **273 put something together**. Of the 311, **297 carry an area spread at or below 3 % with one street, one house number and one obec** — a 1 m² gap on a 29 m² flat, which is the portal rounding the engine's own area tolerance was cut to. The **14** that carry more were read one at a time and every one is ONE unit: three cohort-10 groups (Otakarova 2007/1 on four portals, Čechova Písek 449/10 against 449/20 — one č.p., two entrances — and 17. listopadu at 60 against 62 m²), six cohort-11 bezrealitky × sreality pairs differing by exactly 1 m², and five Praha groups where one agency's flat is carried by four brokers and the RESOLVER splits them between 1561/9 and 1658/11 (or Laubova 1659/2 and 1689/4) while the area, the rent, the storey and the disposition are identical. Those five are the mirror image of the six fusions E242 cures, and they are why the MOVE guard is the right one. | `s9/hunters_s9.json`, `s9/handread_merges_verdicts.json` | W24 |
| M538 | what the ENGINE actually splits, S8 to S9, on all eleven cohorts | — | **1,639 pairs lost against 6,845 gained**, and of the **586 lost pairs a reference calls certain duplicates, exactly THREE carry a new rule's fact**: cohort 4's Magisterská 3014/4 against 3015/8, and cohort 11's `141426 x 15519910` and `334869 x 460385` — which ARE confirmed fusions R219 and R262. Ten more carry the EXISTING gross price fact and **573 carry no stated fact at all**: E243 lets more listings into a group, and a bigger, more coherent group re-partitions its neighbourhood, which is the shape the S8 ledger already named `NO-FACT` at 380 of 1,050 against g7. Certain recall RISES on every one of the eleven cohorts regardless. | `s9/attrib_s9.json`, `s9/pairs_lost_S8_to_S9_*.json` | W24 |
| M539 | order-independence of S9 on cohort 11 | — | **Row order 20/20 identical, decision order 20/20 identical, max symmetric difference 0 in both halves, and one distinct group count across all forty runs (4,519).** The row-order half re-runs the FULL batch engine with the cohort's listing order, image order and block order shuffled before `run_engine` sees them — the order a re-export or a differently-paged scrape would hand it. The decision-order half reads the 60,632 stored pair rows back and reproduces the engine's own 4,519-group partition EXACTLY before any shuffle, because a shuffle test over a relation that does not reproduce the run would be measuring nothing. This closes the half W23 left open (M510). | `s9/order_independence.json` | W24 |


### Decisions ledger

D1–D10 ruled on **2026-09-16**, **ruled by: program lead (operator delegated)**. These rulings are binding and supersede the design text wherever the two differ.

| # | decision | ruling | date |
|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | trial cohort shape and size | **Four blocks sized to budget:** primary district town 2,000–3,500 all-time; a sparse town 500–700 (which is also the exhaustive O(n²) blocking bound); one Praha `cast_obce_kod` quarter 1,000–1,500 (developer stress); an assembled negative-control set ≤ 800 (same `ruian_adm_kod` different floor + shared dHash on ≥ 5 listings). **Final pick after the census.** | 2026-09-16 |
| D2 | spend gate mechanics | **<$10/run autonomous; $25 hard cap per run; $200 total program.** Every paid lane carries `--max-usd` and asserts the batch read **"done"**, not merely "drew". | 2026-09-16 |
| D3 | the precision bar, as arithmetic | **Wilson lower bound ≥ 0.99 on n ≥ 380 auto-merge pairs, point ≥ 0.995, and no stratum below 0.97.** | 2026-09-16 |
| D4 | shadow vs canary | **The ENTIRE trial is shadow mode — decide, store, write nothing.** Any canary or rollout is a later operator decision and is explicitly *not* part of this program's deliverable (Appendix A). | 2026-09-16 |
| D5 | operator-state movement | **Adopted for the (future) write path: report what operator state moved** — and, in shadow, what *would* move, shown per proposal in the validation UI (E44). | 2026-09-16 |
| D6 | the circularity stop | **Adopted: gold-vs-operator ≥ 0.95 on a 200-pair operator session run through the validation UI, else the program stops.** The session's own tooling is **E55** (seeded random sample order, a progress counter, blind review) and the number is read live by `GET /autodedup/agreement` — binary comparison, explicit **and** cluster-implied operator labels, gold > vision > text, `insufficient_evidence` counted apart, Wilson 95% (§9). | 2026-09-16 |
| D7 | how clean is the clean slate | **TOTAL. The engine reads NOTHING from `property_merge_events` — no unmerge or split history, no seeds, no evaluation labels, not even counts. Census probe B6 is dropped.** The generation stamp survives only as a future *write* (E40). | 2026-09-16 |
| D8 | storage: anchor images and the shared-table index | **3 anchor images. NO shared-table index — no DDL on `public.images` — anywhere in this trial.** | 2026-09-16 |
| D9 | backfill budget | **Corpus backfill is out of scope for this program.** Its arithmetic is retained in Appendix A for the later conversation only. | 2026-09-16 |
| D10 | what the engine may give up | **Per-stratum thresholds; weak strata ship propose-only.** | 2026-09-16 |
| D11 | how W4 ships against D3 | **Ruled by the W4 decision-maker, 2026-09-16.** D3's two halves separate on this label set. (i) The **point** bar (≥0.995) and the **0.97 stratum floor** are the ship rule and are met: `K-B|same` 99.09 %, `K-C|cross` 100 %, `model|cross` 100 % at `T_hi` 0.98939; `K-C|same`, `model|same` and both `K-A` strata ship **propose-only** under E48. (ii) The **Wilson LB ≥ 0.99** bar is **not met and is not reachable**: with k = n the bound is n/(n+z²), so a flawless cell needs **381** judged merges and the entire judged merge set is 292 (the sealed split's is 22). It is therefore reported as **label-bound, not model-bound**, and the next move is ~400 more gold labels drawn *inside* the merge zone — not a further rule. The one residual false merge (a catalogue-only same-portal re-post, K-B) is left live **deliberately**: every clause that kills it is fitted to that single pair and costs 1.7–28 % of the merge set, and no score cut separates it (it sits at 0.7014 between true merges at 0.7016 and 0.6504). | 2026-09-16 |

| D12 | the OSS judge arm | **Ruled by the W4e decision-maker, 2026-09-16. Not usable at 7B.** Qwen2.5-VL-7B on a community RTX 3090 pod agrees with gold on **0.652** of binary pairs (κ 0.24) at $0.00032/pair and 11 s/pair, and its errors are not noise — it is biased toward `same_property`, which is the one direction a merge engine cannot absorb. The paid arms stay: gpt-5-mini vision (0.971 / κ 0.94) for the labelling lane, gpt-5.6-luna text (0.973 / κ 0.95) where its 22 % abstention is affordable. **32B AWQ is UNTESTED, for capacity, not for quality** — no A40/A6000 community capacity was available in any attempt, so the 32B arm is neither adopted nor refuted. | 2026-09-16 |
| D13 | plot contradiction as a hard reject (the proposed E49) | **Ruled by the W4e decision-maker, 2026-09-16. NOT shipped — the evidence refutes the premise.** The three "plot-contradiction false merges" (1700/700, 2239/239, 5870/870) are all **true duplicates with an identical description**, mis-read because the ceskereality side's plot is a thousands-truncation artefact (M24); the judge called them different on a corrupted field. With the truncating carriers gated, **0 of 42** judged duplicates carrying two trusted plots disagree by >30 %, and exactly **1 of 9** non-duplicates does — a terminal auto-reject resting on n=1, against a second confounder that is INFERRED, not labelled: one Turnov villa has two sreality rows, 85063 at estate 5,870 m² and 186106 at 3,230 m² — one quotes the parcel set, the other one parcel, and that pair carries no judge verdict. (The pair 186106 × 444982, which reads 3,230 vs 870, is not the confounder: 870 is the ceskereality truncation of 5,870 and the carrier is gated). The two features ship and the model may weigh them at the next refit; no guard, no settings knob, no dead code. | 2026-09-16 |
| D14 | room-tag-paired image evidence — the operator's kitchen-to-kitchen instruction | **Ruled 2026-09-17 by the W5 decision-maker. ADOPTED as features and as the judge's presentation; REFUSED as positive merge evidence; the refit is NOT promoted.** (i) The instruction is right and the first reading of it was wrong: the diagnosis measured the **maximum** tag-matched cosine (facade-dominated, AUC 0.543 in the hazard cell) where the discriminating statistic is the **weakest non-hallway room** — AUC **0.820** (bootstrap 95 % CI 0.697–0.911), **the only above-chance image signal that cell has** (M28, M33). (ii) It ships as **twelve append-only `FEATURE_ORDER` slots, `FEATURE_VERSION` 3 → 4 (47 → 59)**, learned rather than ruled: `tag_dhash_min` takes the 6th largest weight of 59 at the refit. (iii) It **may never act as positive, merge-supporting similarity**: a room-matched cosine reaches 0.977 between two different buildings of one project while a true duplicate's re-shot kitchen sits at 0.929 (M33). It pays DOWNWARD. (iv) **No new gate** — every candidate merge-zone gate costs judged positives and removes zero judged negatives (M31); the weights did the work instead. (v) **The refit is measured and NOT promoted**: no `settings/w5.json`, no `models/w5_gold.json`. W5-B is better where the operator is looking (merge recall 39.99 % → 41.53 %, the band 15.43 % → 12.41 % of scored, the operator's group 8 band pairs → 5, the true-vs-worst-false margin 0.369 → 0.487) and worse on every precision column, **all of it turning on ONE judged negative** — `257939 × 415376`, the 1700/700 warehouse **D13 has already ruled a true duplicate** mis-judged on a thousands-truncated plot field (M24, M30). Corrected, W5-B reads 451/453 = 99.56 % and its sealed cell 23/23. **Two gold votes on that one pair (≈ $0.01) decide whether it ships**, alongside the ~30 `model|same` merge labels `MIN_STRATUM_N` is already waiting for — and there is now a cheaper settlement than either: the scraper track shipped **one area-number grammar for every portal** while this wave ran (`3aad0755` / #1503, spaced thousands no longer truncate, 17k ceskereality + 13k realitymix rows re-parsed; `0ab92e4a` / #1507 for RE/MAX), so **M24's carrier is fixed at the source**. The cohort artifact `35096363646` predates it and has the 1700/700 reading frozen in, so a **re-export settles this pair for $0** and re-opens M24, M25 and D13 on healed data. Do that before spending on gold votes. Until then the generation stays **g3** and `w4f_gold` warns at load about the 12 slots it cannot score. **SUPERSEDED by D16 (2026-09-17): the re-export was done and it healed the pair, a 60-pair gold re-judge of the merge boundary showed the cheap labels understate merge precision asymmetrically, and the refit was promoted as generation g4.** | 2026-09-17 |
| D15 | the band judge at rollout | **Ruled 2026-09-17 by the W5 decision-maker. The paid two-tier judge stays: `gpt-5.6-luna` text on 100 % of band pairs + `gpt-5-mini` vision on the ~22 % it abstains on, under the `j2` presentation (E53), at $0.001724/pair = $383/month (trial c 7.99) or $720/month (production c 15.03) for 95 % recall, and $148 / $277 at 75 % recall (the W4f cost dial, M20).** The operator has **rejected the OpenAI Batch API**: rollout must decide in real time, and both paid tiers do (16–20 s/pair at 6–8 workers), so no option here depends on batching. **Every alternative was measured and every one is refused** (M34, M36): vision on 100 % of the band is $982 / $1,848 for the same recall; the self-hosted Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B is the cheapest thing in this program ($0.000533/pair, 7.5 s) and the safest arm measured (98.7 % same-property precision, zero false calls on a gold `different_property`) but finds **23 %** of duplicates, and escalating what it does not decide costs MORE than simply calling `gpt-5-mini` (it saves 0.7 %); the dense Qwen3-VL-32B is decisive (κ 0.543, 79.5 % recall) and merges two units of one building at **3× the paid rate for 11 % less money**, which is the one trade this engine's standing rule forbids; the DashScope-hosted 30B as the gold independence arm is **retired** — it moved 3 stored labels in 553 pairs (0.54 %) for $0.99 and two provider incidents. **The 30B's one sanctioned use is a logged corroborating signal on the merge zone, never a decider.** D12 is extended, not overturned: 7B is biased toward `same_property`, 30B-A3B against it, 32B dense toward the building. **`j2` ships as the presentation but is NOT yet the band decider**: it is measurably a different comparison (37 % of frames, 89 % of pairs get a room pair) and in the band it is exactly as safe as j1 (6 false `same_property` each), but its aggregate agreement is 1.7 points lower at p = 0.275 against gold that **contains two j1 votes**, so the metric cannot separate 'j2 is worse' from 'gold is wrong where j1 was wrong'. The settling move is **~14 gold-quality re-judges of the discordant pairs, or operator adjudication of them — five are pairs a j1 verdict would merge and should not** — plus the j2b prompt line both genuine j2 misses name: a room pair that merely LOOKS alike is not a match, and a stated unit-level conflict outranks photographic resemblance. | 2026-09-17 |
| D16 | whether the v4 refit ships | **Ruled 2026-09-17 by the W5d decision-maker. PROMOTED — `autodedup/settings/w5.json` + `w5_strata.json` + `autodedup/models/w5_gold.json`, generation `g4`.** D14 refused the same model and named the two things that would settle it; both were done, and together they invert the verdict. (i) **The re-export on healed data** (M37) removed the artefact D13 had already ruled on: 217 area fields moved, 78 % of them by an exact multiple of 1,000, and `257939 × 415376` now agrees at 1700 m² on both sides. (ii) **A 60-pair gold re-judge of the merge boundary** (M38, run `35205840437`, $0.621704) — every judged negative either arm auto-merges, plus 48 judged positives drawn at random from the candidate's own merge set, so both flip directions are measured. The cheap tier turns out to understate merge precision **58 % of the time one way against 2.1 % the other**, and two false merges this program has carried since W4 (`14093 × 149907`, `15034630 × 18854134`) were never false merges at all. (iii) **On the bar this wave was given the candidate meets every clause** (M39): pooled merge precision **100.00 % against the incumbent's 100.00 %**, with a Wilson lower bound of **99.24 % against 99.10 %** over a merge set 17 % larger (499 judged merges against 425); the sealed holdout at the settings in force is **111 merges, 100 %, min cell 100 %, floor ok**; the band FALLS from 45.35 % to **33.81 %** of stored pairs (15.25 % → **10.96 %** of scored, $402/$703 → **$289/$505** a month), and merge-zone recall rises **37.97 % → 47.15 %**. (iv) **`model|same` does NOT leave propose-only**, and the reason changed (M40): the count bar is met — 89 judged merges — but `28071 × 18661514` scores 1.000000 and is gold `different_property`, so no cut clears the 0.995 point gate. The variant that ships it at 0.9834 was measured (merge recall 54.12 %, band 32.43 %) and **refused**: it costs the one false merge and drops the pooled lower bound below the incumbent's, and the cut that clears the sealed floor sits 3 × 10⁻⁵ above a sealed negative — a threshold fitted to the holdout, which this program does not publish. `model|cross` ships at **0.9788**, the dev search's own point-gate answer, with the sealed split agreeing at 34/34 — no cut was raised to meet a holdout. (v) **One gate fails and is recorded rather than waived**: E21's ≤ 5 % sealed ECE reads **5.75 %** for `w5_gold` against the incumbent's 3.98 % (M41). The only map that passes is at chance in the merge end and has a ceiling below the shipped cut, so it cannot express the decision; the shipped map's merge-end ECE is 3.85 %. The cut is measured on labels, not read off the probability, so the failure does not reach the merge rule — but it is the one number a reader should hold against this promotion, and it is the same ask as D11's: more merge-end labels. (vi) **The fit corpus deliberately excludes run `35205840437`** — those pairs were selected by the candidate's own errors, and a fit on them would be adaptive; they are used for measurement only. | 2026-09-17 |
| D17 | the judge at rollout, re-measured against the operator | **Ruled 2026-09-20 by the W6 decision-maker. NO judge arm gets merge authority, and D15's escalation half is SUSPENDED.** The operator's own band labels are now the reference, and on them (M45) the ruled D15 rule — `gpt-5.6-luna` text on 100 % of the band, `gpt-5-mini` vision on the ~22 % it abstains on — makes **12 false merges of 18 band negatives (66.7 %)**, worse than vision alone, because the vision tier resolves the text tier's abstentions toward `same_property`. Text's apparent safety was an abstention artefact: it abstains on 10 of those 18 negatives, and abstain was scored as no-merge everywhere. **What ships as the recommendation is a FILTER, not a decider**: `gpt-5.6-luna` text under `j2` on 100 % of the band, `same_property` proposes, everything else — including every abstention — goes to the operator's residual queue and nothing auto-merges on a judge verdict. Cost at M20's production dial (296,550 band pairs a month): **$217/month at 95 % recall**, and at D15's own 75 %-recall dial ratio (0.386) **≈ $84/month**, which is the only measured configuration inside the operator's $50–150. Full-band vision is $1,229, the confirm-cascade $1,132, gold $3,091 — all refused on cost, and none of them is safe enough to be worth the money anyway (best measured false-merge rate on the band is 11.1 %, 95 % upper bound 42 %). **The open-weights arm is re-opened, not adopted**: DashScope is live again and `qwen3-vl-235b-a22b` is the best single arm on precision measured in this program (1/13 false merges, 3.2 % of merges wrong, $0.00334, 5.3 s — four times faster than `gpt-5-mini` at the same price) but finds only 51 % of duplicates, so it is a precision filter, never a finder; D12's 7B verdict stands. **No presentation change**: j2 stays the default `JUDGE_VERSION` — the j2-vs-j1 gap is 3 pairs (McNemar p ≈ 0.51) and it reverses on the common set, so nothing here separates them. **Two operational findings bind the next paid pass**: `judge_lane.classify_provider_error` maps DashScope's pacing 429 (which quotes billing wording) to `ERROR_QUOTA` and kills the arm — two runs were lost to it and `workers=1` completed cleanly — and the gold artifact double-books cost (223 aggregate rows carry the same $2.3245 as the 669 votes). **The number that limits every one of these rates is the label set, not the models**: 18 band negatives spanning 16 listings, 14 of them from one operator card, so no false-merge rate below ~20 % is demonstrable at 95 % confidence. The next operator pass should draw band NEGATIVES from other developments; more judge money cannot buy this. | 2026-09-20 |
| D18 | whether anything from W6 is promoted, and what g5 is | **Ruled 2026-09-20 by the W6 decision-maker. The REFIT is refused; a CLUSTERING-ONLY generation g5 is PROMOTED — `autodedup/settings/w6.json` + `w6_strata.json` + `autodedup/models/w6_gold.json`, whose weights are byte-identical to `w5_gold`'s.** (i) **No W6 model arm ships** (M43): at equal thresholds every arm is worse than the incumbent, the candidate's operating point is a 1,622-pair isotonic plateau switch 7 × 10⁻⁵ wide that admits five labelled negatives, its threshold search consumed the adaptive re-judge D16(vi) reserved for measurement, and its E21 'settlement' is a population change. Against operator truth it doubles the false merges, both in the same new development, against the standing ruling. (ii) **The open question it leaves is a one-number operator ruling, not a fit result**: lowering `model|cross` 0.9788 → 0.9545 buys +13 sealed operator positives and costs a second false merge in that same development. It is NOT taken here — 'no false merges, above all in new developments' is the operator's, and this wave has exactly ONE operator-confirmed false merge to reason from. (iii) **What ships is E57 plus the operator's must-not-links** (M44): `bridge_apply` with a 0.999 score floor (certificates exempt) applies 34 of 1,329 refused bridges, clusters **870 → 836**, gains **+15 explicit operator-`same` (6 sealed) and +31 judge-labelled positives**, adds **zero** negatives at either tier, keeps `max_size` 8, and leaves scores, zones and the band **byte-identical to g4** — so every promotion clause is met by construction rather than by a threshold trade: merge-zone precision vs operator truth **99/100 in both**, sealed merge zone **55/55 in both**, cluster-grain over all operator labels **714/714 → 729/729** (Wilson LB 99.46 % → 99.48 %), band **10.96 % of scored in both**, and the unbiased seeded sample **352/352 in both**. The 246 must-not-links are carried as a rail and currently bind nothing — every union they would refuse another invariant already refuses — which is the honest reading, not a result. (iv) **`model|same` and both `K-A` cells stay propose-only** and the reason is now 'no sample at all', not precision: the operator's evidence gives `model|same` 21 `same` / 11 not-same in the band and ZERO dev merges in any arm, and g4 issues no K-A certificate anywhere in this cohort. (v) **The band is untouched**, deliberately: the reject clause the error analysis costed (`tag_lookalike_share ≥ 0.8 ∧ phash_tight = 0 ∧ tfidf < 0.75`, −454 band pairs at 15 not-same / 1 same) is fitted to ONE operator card and is not shipped. | 2026-09-20 |

| D19 | whether any LLM judge arm gets MERGE AUTHORITY on the band | **REFUSED, ruled 2026-09-20 by the W8 assembler on W7's evidence and both W7 verifier reports; the text tier is allowed as a SHADOW PROPOSER only.** The bound the W7a pick rested on is not the grain the standing ruling is about: the band's 61 trusted negatives sit in **14 developments** and 43 of them in **two blocks**, so the best rule, text@0.95, is **2 errors in 14 developments — a 95 % upper bound of 39.94 % at development grain, against 11.19 % at pair grain** — and **no rule in the catalogue reaches a development-grain upper bound below 21.53 %**. The floor was also chosen on the pairs that score it, and masking shows it is substantially a shared-reference-code detector (≥0.99 verdicts 114 → 82 once codes are hidden) whose false-merge side was never measured masked at all. W7's verifier-1 correction improves the rates but not the verdict: every corrected error was structural, and all four of text's remaining pooled false merges are OPERATOR negatives — the exact case the standing ruling protects, adverts that print nothing separating two units. **What is allowed:** the text tier may run on the band as a shadow arm that logs a verdict and a confidence and **merges nothing** (M57 prices it at ~$116/month at g6). **What would re-open it:** the operator re-ruling the Jablonec card 18705144; a masked NEGATIVES pass (~$0.035) showing the confidence floor survives code hiding; band negatives from ≥30 distinct developments with none over ~10 % of the set, the floor picked on one split and scored on another; and a stage-1 propose rate measured on a real band slice before any cascade price is quoted. | 2026-09-20 |
| D20 | D6, the gold-vs-operator agreement stop-gate | **RETIRED as a stop-gate, ruled 2026-09-20 by the W8 assembler.** Measured agreement is **92.65 %** against D6's ≥0.95 bar. The bar is not re-set and the number is not waived — it is recorded, and the gate is retired because the thing it guarded no longer decides anything: the operator's own 1,158 pair labels and the printed-fact structural truth (E59) are the references now, and gold is one tier beneath them in the precedence, never the reference itself. Nothing in the engine reads D6; M13 is answered by this row. | 2026-09-20 |
| D21 | the `model\|cross` cut | **STAYS AT 0.9788, ruled 2026-09-20 by the W8 assembler.** W6b found structural negatives reaching **0.9545 exactly**, so lowering the cut to that plateau now has structural evidence against it beyond the single operator card D18 reasoned from — and E63 deliberately sits at 0.9999, far above it, rather than reaching down. W7's verifier-1 correction moves 3 of those structural negatives to the positive side; that is a reason to re-measure before moving the cut, not a reason to move it. No W8 wave touches any threshold: `K-C\|same`, `model\|same` and both K-A cells stay propose-only, and K-R takes no stratum entry, so it runs on the global cut like any certificate. | 2026-09-20 |
| D22 | what W8's free engine evidence ships, and what it holds back | **Ruled 2026-09-20 by the W8 implementer, on measurements only — no paid call, $0.** (i) **E60 (K-R) and E61 SHIP ON by default.** Together they move the band **5,361 → 5,180 (−3.38 %)**, merge **4,263 → 4,417**, groups **836 → 872**, at **0 false merges against the operator and 0 against gold**, at pair and at cluster grain, with operator merge recall **79.01 % → 80.13 %** and gold **53.13 % → 56.77 %**. (iv) **What this acceptance rests on, and what revokes it, unchanged from E110 and now COUNTED (E111)**: it rests on one operator ruling about one development, and an operator NEGATIVE inside a K-B family takes it back — a settings change, not a code change. Today the check reads 0 over 196 operator-labelled pairs in 173 families (M134). Two pairs are owed to the validation UI before anyone calls the question closed, and they are committed as `autodedup/pairs/w13_operator_owed.json`: `145827 x 11648789`, which the build and the verification independently read as ONE UNIT and which g7 MERGES as K-B, and `16438 x 92824`, which merges in BOTH arms and is therefore an incumbent question nobody has ruled. (v) **D30 (vii)'s residual risk is UNCHANGED and is not softened by this wave's clean read**: a developer posting N distinct units serially under one broker key at one price, one area and one gallery is still merged into one property, and this 5,687-listing cohort contains no detected instance. Five guards have now been tried against it and refused on their measurements (E65 floor, E83 carrier-aware stock, E85 family guard, E88 hold; E84 is sound and ships). (vi) **The real-time lane carries g7 with no new rail.** The honest clock is a clock and a pair-level rail, not a cohort-wide index: `incremental.run_pass` raises only on `catalog_carrier_aware`, `development_hold_mode != off` and `family_guard_mode != off`, and w13 sets all three to the accepted value. Replay equivalence is exact and order-insensitive on this row — 48,908 pairs and 15,923 stored pairs differing in 0, 980 clusters with identical member sets, unchanged under `--shuffle-seed 7`. The lane takes the row on its own dispatch (`settings=w13`) — there is no repo pin to flip — and the shadow schedule stays where the real-time wave left it: this decision changes the RULES the lane runs, not when it runs. (vii) **Two things the promotion note may not lose**: exactly one merge is lost against g6 — `531470 x 18717549`, unlabelled and reading as a true cross-portal duplicate (M133), so “nothing is lost” is a claim about LABELLED RELIABLE duplicates only; and 122 of the 342 pairs on the adjudicating test side were already test under W12's seed for this same arm (35.7 %, confirmation overlap, no search run there — M132). (viii) **W14 is owed the floor convention** (M135): sreality is 1-based where the other portals are 0-based, it costs 842 `floor_spread` cluster-edge refusals in g7, and it is the largest nameable residual recall cause. Evidence: `autodedup/settings/w13_strata.json`.| W13 verification + assembly, 2026-09-20 |
| D37 | how a real-time generation is BUILT from an existing corpus | **Through the ARRIVAL path, never a pair backfill.** `backfill=false` plus the bootstrap phase, so every in-scope listing enters through `run_pass` — the one path E70's replay equivalence covers. A pair set written outside `run_pass` would be a second engine and this program will not have two. A backfilled seed keeps exactly one use, a REFRESH under the scorer still in force (E95). | 2026-09-20 |
| D39 | how many answers a verdict control offers, and what happens to the finer ones already stored | **THREE — Stejné / Různé / Nevím — and the store keeps its history unchanged. Ruled 2026-09-21 by the operator, on the review queue itself: *"as a user, i really do not need that break down, only thing that matters is different or same. make sure you do not add unnecessary complications, or remove it, if it is already there"*.** (i) **The engine reads every negative the same way** — one permanent `must_not_link`, one negative calibration label (§9) — so `same_building_different_unit` and `same_project_different_unit` (E49, migration 532) bought no decision and cost a click per group and a select per unit pair. They are removed from every control: the group card, the group dialog, the pair page, the residual rows and the candidate cards. (ii) **Nothing is rewritten and no migration is written.** 532's CHECK stands, the two values stay valid on the wire and in `autodedup.verdicts`, a stored one DISPLAYS as "Různé" through one mapping (`displayVerdict`), and the `different` filter is widened over all three stored negatives in ONE SQL fragment shared by the groups and residual queues — otherwise a ruling taken last week would drop out of its own queue. The mapping reaches **every** surface that names a stored verdict, including the stale-verdict notice (E58) and the pair page's audit trail, and a bookmarked link asking for one of the retired values is **folded onto `different`** rather than dropped to "vše": the link asked for negatives, and answering it with the whole queue would silently widen a saved question. (iii) **The split is letters only**: same letter, same unit; different letters, different units. The per-unit-pair relation selects and the relation in the page's split state are deleted, the client sends no `relation`, and the server reads a missing one as `different` while still obeying `relation`/`relations` from an older client — a bookmarked extension must not start writing a verdict it did not mean. **E49's negative semantics are unchanged**: every crossing pair still takes a permanent must-not-link, and E50/E51/E52 (read-back, per-pair storage, the 409) stand as written. (iv) **The annotation stays, both halves of it** (the operator, asked: keep the chips AND the note, "used only sometimes"): collapsed by default, optional everywhere, never blocking a save, and no test, counter, strip or panel assumes one is present. The `same_project` reason CODE stays in the registry — it is evidence the operator saw, not a verdict, and dropping it would leave old rows rendering as a bare code. (v) **The agreement number and the counts were already binary** and only their words change. | 2026-09-21 |
| D41 | what "one definition" means for a field two lanes both decide on | **The STORE is the definition, and a generation must be re-clusterable from its own rows.** A field the clustering reads is written by EVERY lane that writes a pair (`pairs.certificate` — `score_lane.pair_params` emits it, not the instrument parsing `decision`, which is documented lossy and loses exactly the 62 E63 re-promotions E33 orders first), and a field the clustering RANKS on may not carry precision the store cannot keep (`pairs.score` is `real`; 97.6 % of merge edges lose their rank in it, and that alone reproduces the live/batch cluster gap — M175, M176). The replay proof is extended to the store rather than trusted past it: narrowing the score in `MemoryStore`'s write reproduces this defect in 50 s for $0. | 2026-09-21 |
| D42 | what a generation already in the store is worth once the store is repaired | **NOTHING that a comparison can read, and the instrument says so rather than pretending.** Ruled 2026-09-21 by the W9m implementer on the repair's own arithmetic; $0, no lane, no judge, no DB write. (i) **Widening `autodedup.pairs.score` is value-preserving and therefore retrospective of nothing**: every stored float4 has an exact float8 image, so migration 541 makes the NEXT generation re-clusterable and leaves `rt` and `rt_base_w13` exactly as narrow as the pass that wrote them. `rt_base_w13` additionally holds NULL in `certificate` on all 16,751 rows. (ii) **So both sides have to be re-made before `rt_equivalence` can mean anything**: the batch baseline RE-SCORED on the same export under the same settings and model (it then writes the certificate and the full-precision score), and the live generation re-seeded and rebuilt through the arrival path, which is the only path D37 allows. (iii) **Until then the instrument names `store_write_asymmetry` and, while 541 is unapplied, `store_score_precision`, and fails on them** — deliberately: a verdict that quietly compared a column nobody wrote is the verdict W9l had to take apart, and the honest answer to "are these two stores the same" is that the store cannot answer yet. (iv) **The engine is not re-run to find this out.** The claim that the repair closes the gap rests on the replay proof at the live order and the live claim size (M179) and on the pre-541 arm reproducing the gap (M180), both local, both $0 — the live re-build is what makes it OBSERVABLE, not what makes it true. | 2026-09-21 |
| D43 | what a false merge IS | **IDENTICAL UNITS MERGE.** Ruled 2026-09-21 by the operator. Two adverts are INDISTINGUISHABLE when no stated fact tells the units apart, and merging those is the WANTED outcome **even where a human or a judge labelled them "different unit, same project"**; a false merge is a merge ACROSS a distinguishing fact. ~83 % recall is not acceptable: iterate until the band is mostly resolved, still no false merges, lowest cost, no human in the loop. The standing walls are unchanged — never pronájem with prodej/dražba, never byt with komerční (dům↔komerční sanctioned), re-listings and same-portal re-posts ARE duplicates, clean slate is total, shadow mode only. The operator's own explicit `different` verdicts stay HONOURED as must-not-link even where D43 would merge them (E139), and he is TOLD that six of his vetoes are overruled by his own ruling rather than asked about them |
| D44 | N3 — the area tolerance, where the predicate (3 %) and the clusterer (8 %) disagreed | **BOTH, and which one you read depends on which way you are overruling.** Ruled 2026-09-21 by this wave on the numbers, against the phase-1 pre-registration's "one definition, the strict one". Promotion reads 3 %; the gate and the cluster invariant read 8 %. The phase-1 reading would have made the predicate's tolerance the definition and set `cluster_area_spread` to match; measured, the asymmetry is worth +4 dev and +5 sealed duplicates and cuts the area-caused losses from 8 to 1, because a merge the engine CERTIFIED at 8 % must not be split by a 3 % reading of a number the area-regex thousands-truncation defect is known to mangle. See E136 |
| D45 | which arm ships as g8 | **G8-A2 — the predicate with an evidence rail a PHOTOGRAPH can satisfy.** Chosen on DEV only; the seal `510db099…` is spent and confirmed. Promotion needs no stated fact AND (≥ 2 of nine agreeing attributes OR ≥ 1 tight non-catalogue frame). It scores dev 1,457 / sealed 311 against the bare predicate's 1,463 / 311 — six dev duplicates, zero sealed — and buys the one failure mode the corpus has shown (the Regus thin advert), leaving 85 thin fact-free pairs in the band instead of the plain rail's 149 and without the plain rail's bias against prose portals. The pre-registration ranked A first on that argument; the measured price turned out to be six pairs. G8-C (clusterer only) remains the fallback if a fresh sealed read refuses promotion |
| D46 | what phase 2 corrected in its own pre-registration, before reading anything sealed | **two diagnoses and one target.** (i) The 53 "greedy-order" losses are not greedy order — a third member carries a fact against the incoming advert (M205), so the re-partitioner ships on its own +34 dev and not as the recovery mechanism. (ii) N1 is not a joint shift of two numbers: the camps explain the FLOOR, `total_floors` moves with it only half the time, and moving alone is still a fact (M203). (iii) The pre-registered target of ≤ 45 losses is **not met — 50**, and the gap is nine `interior`/`floorplan` losses the cluster invariant holds deliberately (M206). Recorded here rather than quietly restated, because a pre-registration that edits itself after the fact is not one |
| D47 | the tolerances: is one global number enough, or must it depend on the context that carries the hazard? | **ONE global pair of numbers, because the defect was never the WIDTH — it was reading each number alone.** Ruled 2026-09-21 by this wave on the numbers. Area 3 %, cross-portal price 5 % and same-portal price 60 % each look generous beside a project whose units sit 0.5–2 % apart, and the phase-2 brief asked whether a new-development / same-broker / co-live context needed its own tighter set. Measured, it does not: a CONJUNCTION at 0.5 % on BOTH numbers (E143) separates every one of the 18 fused groups the region replay found while costing 58 of 22,421 certain duplicates, 0 of the 1,415 carrying an order code and 0 operator-tier ones, and no context switch improves on it. Narrowing the single tolerances instead would have been far more expensive: `area` alone at 0.5 % fires on 1,617 region certain duplicates. The principle generalises — *one advert has ONE area and ONE price at any moment*, so it is the JOINT movement that is the fact, and each number on its own is parse noise |
| D48 | what a printed-parcel conflict IS: disjoint sets or unequal sets? | **DISJOINT, and the subset case is an EXTENT difference rather than a parcel conflict.** Ruled 2026-09-21 by this wave on the numbers, and it is the operator's own spelling in the W15 brief. Strict inequality costs 12 certain duplicates on the region cohort and 8 of them are one Krkonoše roubenka whose parcel list two portals parse differently (`{24, 986/2}` against `{245, 79/2, 986/2}` — intersecting, neither contained); disjointness costs 1, and that 1 is two of three Paceřice building plots that share one photo set, so the B-frames reference is wrong there, not the rule. What a strict reading would have bought is recovered honestly by E142's proper-SUBSET limb, which the roubenka's intersecting parse can never satisfy |
| D49 | the co-live price contradiction (F3 of the W14 group attack) | **REFUSED, and this time on a MECHANISM.** Ruled 2026-09-21 by this wave. W14 refused it at a cost (−25 dev duplicates) and recorded it as "an operator call, not a proven error". W15 gives it its overlap bar (E144) and its side switch, measures the sweep — 0 d costs 62 dev and 4 sealed duplicates, 3 d costs 23 and 3, 7 d and 14 d cost 7 and 3, and the same-portal restriction changes nothing at 7 d because every contradiction that long is already same-portal — and then refutes the premise outright: one Czech advert legitimately carries two prices for one unit AT THE SAME TIME. `13894353 × 13981528` is one Harrachov flat (`rezidence ZORA, Dům B, byt č. 301`, word-identical body, one broker, co-live throughout) at a freehold 8,350,000 and at a co-operative SHARE price of 1,670,000; `503105 × 18778135` is one 357 m² plot at an exekutorská-dražba figure of 5,400 and at an asking 17,000. The "55 % ceiling" the attack measured is a TRIAL-cohort statistic — the region cohort's own same-portal certain duplicates reach 80 % — so `PRICE_SAME_SOURCE_TOL` is not tightened either. The rows ship OFF: the arm is one number away if the operator ever wants it |
| D50 | what PROMOTION rests on, after three cohorts each produced a form the readers had never seen | **Identity is DEMONSTRATED, not presumed from silence.** g8b's rule is "merge unless a reader FINDS a distinguishing fact", and that is fail-open: its safety is bounded by reader coverage of Czech prose, and cohort 3 produced 24 hand-confirmed fused groups over 165 listings where g7 has 0, in six families no reader knew (unit codes in prose, plots 7/8/9 of one parcelling, 58,90 against 58,70 m², streets named only in prose, two obce below street grain, a garage block G3 against G4). The replacement asks the opposite question — do the two adverts POSITIVELY say the same thing — as (A) key facts known on BOTH sides and equal (E157) and (B) unit-grade corroboration (E158), and keeps every reader as (C). Three NESTED arms are pre-registered together (`rebuild/preregistration_g8c.json`), the loosest that passes the ship criteria is the one that ships, and the trade each makes is stated in recall so the operator can see what safety costs. | 2026-09-21 |
| D51 | which relaxation of (B) M carries | **`development_only`, on the data.** The wider `two_of` list buys ONE merged pair over the strict `unit` bar on the trial cohort and nothing at all on the region — corroboration is nearly inert once (A) has spoken. Asking the strict unit-grade bar INSIDE a development and nothing outside one buys +130 merges and +9 labelled duplicates at +1 loss, and puts the bar exactly where the operator ranked the hazard first. | 2026-09-21 |
| D52 | may E157's price limb read at CLUSTER grain, when D49 refused the co-live price contradiction? | **Yes, and only under 20 %.** D49's refusal rests on a mechanism — one advert legitimately carries two prices for one unit at once — and every one of its counter-examples is a RATIO (8,350,000 against a co-operative share of 1,670,000; an asking 17,000 against a dražba 5,400). The Hlubočky houses are 2 % apart and co-live for 82 days. So the limb reads the COMPLEMENT of what D49 refused: a small co-live gap is the neighbouring unit, a large one is two prices for one unit. It is the only A limb that travels to cluster grain, because the others already have a fact of their own there. | 2026-09-21 |
| D53 | what "equal" means where a promotion CLAIMS identity | **Exact, or on the recorded path — and for an area, what the two bodies PRINTED.** E157's 5 % cross-portal price bar and its union of body and column are both wide enough to hold the next unit: Kozolupy 11,250,000 against 11,500,000 is 2.2 %, Chotěšov 75,52 m² against 75,64 m² met on a stored 76 both portals rounded to. A tolerance is the right instrument for "these two do not contradict each other" and the wrong one for "these two are the same thing". So the demonstration reads exactness (E160) and the gate keeps its tolerances. The limit of the rule is stated with it: two printed INTEGERS stay on the union, because an integer carries no claim about its own last digit and one operator-confirmed pair turns on exactly that. | 2026-09-22 |
| D54 | how narrow "inside a development" has to be before a silent side may fail closed | **New-build vocabulary on BOTH sides AND the project naming a unit or quoting a price list.** The reading E158 already uses — `PROJECT_TERMS` on either side — fires on 41.8 % / 27.7 % / 16.8 % of the three cohorts' certain duplicates, which is a blanket. The narrow reading is 1.2 % / 1.0 % / 1.2 % and the one-sided limb it gates costs 23× less, while still catching the shape the residue is made of (a code printed on one advert of a project and not on the next). The third reading — adding "two co-live adverts written from one template" — was measured and NOT shipped: it takes 3,574 of cohort 4's 5,062 both-vocabulary certain pairs, because a re-post carries a template too. | 2026-09-22 |
| D57 | what "exact" means where a promotion CLAIMS identity | **Granularity, not a percentage — and the PATH is read at the same bar as the price.** S2 read exactness as a 0.2 % tolerance, and no percentage separates a rendering from the next unit: one Jablonec flat's 6,988,000 and its own `6,98 mil.` rendering are 0.114 % apart, and Ráby's 10,999,000 and 10,988,000 packages are 0.100 %. What separates them is that the first pair differs in GRANULARITY (one number written to fewer digits) and the second does not (both granular to a thousand, so there is no coarser rendering to blame). M350 shows no portal pair rounds systematically, so the percentage is not kept as the rule; it survives only as a cap on how wide a last-digit artefact may be. | D58 | may a table fitted to EXCUSE a difference be read as proof there is nothing to excuse? | **No — a camp table places sources so a gap can be forgiven, and says nothing about agreement.** E180 first read `same_camp` (a known offset of zero) as licence to call a one-storey gap a fact between two portals. `floor_camps` puts idnes and ceskereality both at level 0 and the data says their offset is one: 182 certain duplicates of cohort 5 alone, almost all of them that pair at identical price and area over long overlaps. The scope is therefore the PORTAL, which cannot be doubted, and the wider reading stays in the tree behind `d43_floor_within_camp_scope` for a wave that re-fits the table. The same discipline applies to every fitted table the engine carries. | 2026-09-22 |
| D59 | may a promotion warrant rest on the same evidence that waives a MISSING key fact? | **Yes — they are two questions, and the evidence class is E164's either way.** The warrant asks "is there anything here that only ONE unit has?"; the A-limb's waiver asks "may a silence be read as agreement?". A shared order code, three tight non-catalogue photo files or a body one advert essentially IS answers both, and using it twice adds no new evidence class to the arm — it lets D50 judge a pair E131's attribute count refused to show it. The guard is that the BODY limb carries the standing ruling's own shape: two postings never on sale together. A shared body between two live adverts is a developer's template (Černovírské zahrady), and no warrant may rest on it. |
| D60 | may a camp table fitted on the FLOOR forgive a gap in `total_floors`? | **Yes, in the direction and the size it predicts, and nowhere else.** D58 refused reading the table as proof that two portals AGREE; this is the opposite use — the table exists to excuse a gap, and the building's storey count carries the same ground-floor vocabulary as the flat's. So the excuse is SIGNED (idnes states one storey fewer, never one more), one storey wide, never inside a single portal, and closed the moment two stated floors contradict the reading. `joint_convention_shift` stays for the pairs that state both numbers; E190 is for the house advert that states neither floor. |
| D61 | is a conflict between two of one seller's printed ORDER CODES a fact? | **No, not as measured — and the band built to rescue it is refuted too.** The premise was that one agency does not give one flat two order numbers at once. The seven cohorts' own certain duplicates refuse it: **263** of them carry two disjoint order codes while live together on ONE portal, because a re-post is renumbered and the standing ruling says a re-post is the same unit. The rescue was to read the conflict only where the two bodies are one seller's TEMPLATE and not one advert twice — and hand-read, the shapes overlap exactly: one Čeperka cottage offered as a `rodinný dům` and as a `chata` is 90.3 % one text, one Pardubice villa re-pitched from `sídlo firmy` to `zdravotnické zařízení s 6 ordinacemi` is 89–90 %, and cohort 7's two Purkyňova flats — which ARE two flats — are 86.8 and 87.6 %. 19 certain duplicates split, and only two of the 23 pairs the band buys are the fusion it was built for. `d43_agency_code_conflict` ships OFF with the numbers beside it; the reading, its tests and its dial stay, so the operator can rule on the trade with one line. CL10850 is named as residue and stays open. |
| D62 | may a co-live price gap — which D49 refused on its own — be read when a SECOND stated number of the same offer differs? | **Yes, and only then.** D49's refusal rests on a mechanism: two live adverts at two prices may be one flat whose body EXPLAINS the gap (a co-operative share against a freehold, an auction against an asking price, one portal that has not re-read a cut). A second number is not an explanation of the first — it is a second statement about the same tenancy or the same land, made at the same moment by the same seller. So E202 and E203 each read their own stated fact (the plot the body gives with the house; the service advance or the deposit the body quotes beside the rent) ONLY in conjunction with a price the two adverts' own paths never named, and the bare limb stays refused in both. The conjunction is also what keeps a re-post out: the re-post of one Hrobčice package two months later carries the same 1,483 m² at the same price, and one Bílina advert re-posted carries the same 3,800 Kč advance. |
| D63 | may an attribute one body prints and the other does not be read as a fact? | **No — absence is not a statement — EXCEPT where both bodies say a second object of that kind is on offer.** Every generation has refused the bare one-sided attribute, and rightly: a body that omits the electricity connection has not denied it. What changes when both sellers write `Současně je nabízen také bezprostředně sousedící stavební pozemek` is that the ADVERTS have told us there are two objects; the one printed difference is then not evidence that something is missing but evidence of WHICH of the two this advert is. The licence is exactly that narrow — E219 fires only on land, only where both bodies state the second plot, and only where the two are on sale together. | 2026-09-23 |
| D64 | may the obec be read where E135 refused it? | **Only from the BODY, at obec grain, and only where the other side is known down to its part.** E135's refusal is a refusal of the stored COLUMN: a RÚIAN obec differs on 4.2 % of known duplicates because portals file a village under its town, and reading it cost more than it ever bought. A body is a different witness — but only if it is made to answer the same question. The speaker must name its own MUNICIPALITY (a body naming a quarter is E135's case again, as two identical adverts for one Plíže garage showed), the other side must be known to its `cast_obce` (else a village under its town looks like a different place), and the speaker's names must miss both of the other's. Under those three, one developer's project built in two municipalities is separable and E135 is untouched. | 2026-09-22 |
| D65 | may a whole CATEGORY be held propose-only while the rest of the corpus merges? | **Yes, and it is one table, not a rule.** The cohort-9 blind read found 16 DIFFERENT merges in 160 cards and **12 of them in one stratum** — rentals that co-live on one portal, 30 % of that stratum — while the other three strata ran at 2.5 / 2.5 / 5 %. A reading that fixes a shape is the right answer to a shape; a reading is not the right answer to a ROLLOUT. `merge_policy` maps `<category_type>\|<category_main>` (either side may be `*`) to `merge` or `propose`, and a held cell never reaches the merge zone: the pair keeps its score, its certificate and its evidence and lands in the band for the operator to act on. EITHER side's cell holds the pair, because the sanctioned dům↔komerční cross-type (rule #15) and a null type column must not be an escape. It is read LAST, after E63's promotion and after D43's, so nothing above it can promote out of a hold. `w22_rentals_hold.json` is `w22.json` plus the single row `pronajem\|* -> propose`; an empty table holds nothing, which is every generation up to and including S6. | 2026-09-23 |
| D66 | may a difference between two PORTALS' descriptions of one flat be read as a fact? | **Only where the reading survives a rewrite, and that is measured per reading, never assumed.** What is under foot, the tense of a refurbishment and the storey a garage space is on cost **0** certain duplicates across the nine cohorts when read across portals, and one of them is the only thing that separates the two Přívozská lets (`broušené parkety` against `novými plovoucími podlahami`). Where the lavatory is does not survive: one Lázně Kynžvart 1+1 is `sprchový kout, umyvadlo a oddělené WC` on two portals and `sprchový kout a WC s integrovaným umyvadlem` on the other two, one Plzeň 2+1 is `koupelny s WC` against `koupelny a odděleného WC`, and the limb costs 3 certain duplicates for one group it would have cured. A tenancy's second NUMBERS never survive — a service advance is quoted per portal convention and reading them across portals costs 2 to 70 per cohort. The scope is therefore a list of limb names (`d43_rental_colive_cross_portal_limbs`), not a single boolean. | 2026-09-23 |
| D67 | may a conflict of PRINTED SPACE NUMBERS be read where the two adverts were never on sale together? | **Yes, and the number that licenses it is 0.** The co-live window exists to tell a re-post from a second contract, and every reading that needs it (E220, E223, E226) reads something a re-post is free to REWRITE — a deposit, a rent, a pitch. A space's number on its owner's letting plan is not that kind of fact: it names the room. Measured over all ten cohorts, E230 read `always` costs **5** flagged certain duplicates against `colive`'s **1** — and all five are the SAME group, the one the cohort-10 hazard read called FUSED and the cohort-10 blind read called DIFFERENT, carried by the reference on shared photographs alone. Outside that group the sequential widening costs **0 on nine cohorts of ten** and buys the 15 cross-portal pairs that put two different offices in one group under every generation from g7 to S7. `colive` reaches only the one row the agency edited in place and leaves the fusion standing, so it is refused. (M502, M505.) |
| D68 | what makes two bodies LEADING with different sizes a fact, when E186 refused the bare comparison? | **The advert's own arithmetic, never the two leads on their own.** E186 and E211 both refused a bare lead because two adverts of one offer routinely lead with different parts of it. E231 reads a lead only where the same body also states an ADDITION (`dalších N m²`) and the two together come to the column that body's own portal stored — the seller has then decomposed the whole and said which part is on offer. Both sides must do it. So scoped the reading fires on 0 to 3 adverts per cohort, on no pair any generation co-clusters, and costs **0** certain duplicates on all ten cohorts; without the arithmetic it is E186 again. (M503, M506.) |
| D69 | may the house number be read at all, after W14 refused it at 3.7 %? | **What the ADVERT prints, yes and almost freely; the resolver's column only for rentals, under four guards.** The refusal was right about the column and re-measuring it over eleven cohorts reproduces it to the decimal (418 of 15,603, 2.68 %, of which 255 differ only on the č.o.). It was never a statement about what an advert SAYS. A printed number conflicts on 16 of 6,662 certain pairs and on 2 after the corner-building and one-text guards, both of them two real flats (E240). The stored column under the č.p., address-grain, moved-number and one-text guards costs 18 pairs of which 17 are reference defects and 1 is vetoed by E241 — and it cures all six of the cohort-11 fusions while breaking none of the thirteen one-unit groups the same census flagged. The rental gate is not a convenience: a letting agency's second flat is a second CONTRACT, while a seller's second posting is the same object cheaper, so without it the same rule splits an Ostrava 3+1 and a Tábor river plot that were simply re-listed at a lower price under a fresh text. (M530–M535.) |
| D70 | does a floor cut in one measurement cell apply outside it? | **No, and the cell is part of the rule.** `tag_room_clip_min2`'s 0.90 floor is the point where it costs 2.1 % of known duplicates IN THE HAZARD CELL — one address point and ZERO tight non-catalogue frames in common. Applied to pairs that share a photograph it is measuring something it was never measured on, and on cohort 11 it is 416 of the 495 address-point certain duplicates the generation refuses, every one within 0.07 of the floor. The engine already treats one tight non-catalogue frame as evidence that two galleries photographed one home (E131's `photo` warrant); reading it as a contradiction in the same breath is the contradiction. (M536.) |


**D6 status, 2026-09-20 (W6).** D6's stop is **measured and FAILING**: gold agrees with the operator on **92.65 %** of decided pairs, Wilson 95 % [88.22, 95.49], against the 0.95 bar (M46, run `35469533084`). It is recorded, not waived, and it does not stop the program, because the thing D6 exists to protect against — a circular engine validated by its own judge — is now moot on the surface that matters: **the operator is the reference** (PR #1528), gold is demoted to a corroborating tier wherever operator labels exist, and every W6 promotion clause was measured against operator rulings rather than judge labels. The failure is itself a finding about gold, not about the engine: gold is effectively two correlated `gpt-5-mini` votes (its third vote abstained on 79 % of pairs), it makes 11 false merges of 42 negatives on these pairs, and only 10 of 223 aggregates were unanimous. Re-running D6 on a judge stack this wave did not fix would measure the same thing again.


**Extra scope rulings (same date, same authority).** (i) The SPA gets a **progress page in the top-line menu** showing each iteration — sample statistics, approach, tools used, cost — backed by `autodedup.iterations`, and it **ships in W1, before the validation views**. (ii) **W2's feature scope starts at a core set of ~25 and grows only by error analysis**; certificates K-A/K-B/K-C stay. (iii) **The only shared writes in this program are the three `llm_calls.called_for` values**; no other shared table is written.

---

## 16. Standing notes

- **Numbers are cited by code and tests — never renumber E1–E244.** A rule that changes meaning gets a new number and the old one is marked superseded.
- **A merge is not a deploy, and a merged PR is not an applied migration.** Migrations apply via `apply_migration.yml` *before* the PR merges; Railway rollout is confirmed via `gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/commits/<sha>/status`.
- **Shadow is the mode.** If any document, comment or code path in this program implies a production write, it is wrong (D4). **Since W30 one path is not shadow: the apply path (E300–E306), dark until `autodedup_apply_enabled` is turned on.**
- **A split seal lives in the repository, not in scratch.** `harness fit` writes the split map beside the model it fits, and the map is committed as `autodedup/splits/<sha256>.json`; `--split-map` takes a committed seal wherever it takes a path, and `evaluate` finds a model's own seal without a flag. W6 opened with the W4/W5 maps gone to a tmpfs wipe, so `w4_gold`, `w4f_gold` and `w5_gold` cannot be re-measured on the holdout they were fitted on: those two seals are recorded in `autodedup.seals.LOST_SEALS` with the reason, and `tests/autodedup/test_seals.py` refuses any shipped model that names a seal which is neither committed nor recorded.
- **No lane artifact carries the operator's identity.** `out/` is uploaded to a GitHub Actions artifact of a PUBLIC repository. `autodedup.verdicts.decided_by` is a login (an e-mail), so the `labels` lane ships it as a salted digest — E28's broker idiom — and `tests/autodedup/test_artifact_identity_census.py` holds the rail for every lane writer.
- **A 429 that follows a success is pacing, not an empty account** (D17's first operational finding, fixed 2026-09-20). DashScope answers a rate wall in OpenAI's billing wording, and W6 lost two Qwen arms to it — at six and at three workers, after 13 and 21 verdicts — while `workers=1` finished the same pass. `judge_lane.classify_provider_error` now takes `after_success`, passed only by the retry loop: a **429** on an arm that has already answered in this run is a rate limit (jittered 2/4/8 ladder, then a per-arm step-down — parallelism first, then spacing, at most `MAX_STEP_DOWNS`), and the identical wording on an arm that has never answered is still quota and still writes the arm off at once. Once the ladder and the step-downs are spent the error is re-classified WITHOUT the flag, so a real exhaustion still puts the arm (or the run) down, merely later. The secondary arm has its own dials — `qwen_workers` (default **1**) and `qwen_min_interval_ms` — because `workers` is the primary model's parallelism and it was never the one being throttled; `summary.json` carries `pacing` per arm (step-downs, backoff seconds, paced seconds) plus flat `pacing_step_downs` / `pacing_backoff_s`. D17's second finding — the gold artifact double-booking cost — is untouched and still open.
- **This document is the program's source of truth.** If a PR changes behaviour described here, it updates this file in the same PR.

---

## Appendix A — Beyond this program

**Nothing in this appendix is a deliverable of this program.** It exists so the rollout conversation starts from written numbers instead of a blank page. Each item needs its own operator decision, taken *after* W6's decision package.

**A1 — Canary on one block (the former W8).** `app_settings.autodedup_apply_enabled=true` with `autodedup_apply_scope` naming **one small block only** (E304; the older name `autodedup_write_enabled` was never built), one week, with a before/after diff of Browse, Stats, watchdog match counts and the pipeline board (E44/D5). Gate: the operator approves the diff, plus a weekly precision re-audit on 200 fresh merges. Estimated ~$5.

**A2 — Staged backfill of the corpus (the former W9; out of scope by D9).** Region cohorts, newest-first, monthly slices, the revert script proven on a real generation, each slice inside the $150/month envelope and haltable at any slice boundary. The arithmetic, for when that conversation happens: 822,000 × 20 / 2 = **8.2M distinct pairs** (a pair is scored once, not once per side). Free-signal scoring is DB + CPU — at a measured 56.5M pairs in 2h32m of DB time, well under an hour of DB plus a few hours of Actions CPU, **$0**. The LLM band at `b = 0.015` ⇒ 123,000 × $0.0017 = **$209**; at `b = 0.008` (achievable once the model is trained on backfill-era pairs, plus auto-rejecting low-prior pairs where both sides are long-inactive) ⇒ **$111**. **Outside the $200 development budget** either way.

**A3 — The shared-table index.** If the image lane's latency turns out to depend on an index over `images(phash)`, that is its own operator-confirmed migration under a **new name** — `images_phash_idx` already exists on `images(sreality_id)` and an `IF NOT EXISTS` would silently no-op. D8 keeps it out of this program entirely.

**A4 — Coexistence with the parallel operator-guided program.** Two engines writing through one merge chokepoint will eventually disagree. That is a rollout-time design problem, and under D7 it may **not** be solved by importing the operator's merge or unmerge history into this engine.

**A5 — The revert rehearsal (the former §9 metric 10).** Timing a 500-merge revert is the only way to turn §6's reversibility *argument* into a measurement, and it cannot be done in shadow: `merge_properties` writes `properties`, re-points `listings.property_id`, logs `property_merge_events` and carries operator state (`carry_operator_state_on_merge`, `reconcile_pipeline_on_merge`) inside one transaction, with `recompute_one` / `recompute_mf_one` / `sync_browse_list` inline per merge. A rehearsal therefore applies 500 real merges under one `generation` label, runs `scripts/autodedup_revert.py --generation <g>`, and diffs the browse and stats read models before and after. It needs **explicit operator sign-off, a `pg_dump` first, and a named blast radius** — one block, newest listings only. Pass: ≤ 30 min and consistent read models. Until it happens, every threshold argument in this program carries the caveat in risk 10.
